## 2026-06-19：Monitoring Detail / Process Manager MVP

基线提交：
`2e185f5` (`docs: sync current status documents`)

阶段目标：
在现有 Monitor 系统上挂接 Process Manager MVP，让左侧 TOP 进程行和顶部菜单可以打开详细进程列表、进程详情和受保护的进程操作浮窗，同时不改 SSH terminal、SFTP/SCP、Tunnel、Docker、Local Terminal 或 workspace runtime 架构。

### 已实现

- 新增 typed process domain contracts，覆盖进程列表、详情、信号操作、watcher start/stop 和 process events。
- 新增 `internal/processmanager`，包含 Linux 进程采集、解析、排序、过滤、详情查询、受保护信号发送和 watcher 生命周期。
- 进程采集使用独立 SSH exec，不复用 terminal、SFTP/SCP、Tunnel、Docker 或 Local Terminal session。
- 进程列表优先使用 `/proc`，并支持 `ps -eo ...`、BusyBox/Toybox fallback 解析。
- 新增 typed Wails API：
  - `ListProcesses`；
  - `GetProcessDetail`；
  - `SignalProcess`；
  - `StartProcessWatch`；
  - `StopProcessWatch`。
- 新增 process events：
  - `process:state`；
  - `process:list`；
  - `process:detail`；
  - `process:error`。
- 新增前端 process store，按 server/watch 隔离并过滤 late events。
- 新增 `ProcessManagerDialog.vue`，支持进程列表、搜索、排序、详情、默认折叠的 cmdline、手动刷新、实时刷新和 SIGTERM / SIGKILL 二次确认。
- 左侧 compact TOP 进程行可点击，并打开进程管理且选中该 PID。
- 顶部 workspace 菜单新增 `进程管理`。
- 无 active server 时打开进程管理会显示中文 toast：`请先连接并选择一个服务器`。
- 关闭进程管理浮窗会停止对应 watcher。
- `DisconnectServer(serverID)` 会停止该服务器的 process watchers，这是新增 attachment cleanup，不改变服务器级 disconnect 语义。
- Backup 测试新增验证：process runtime state 不进入 backup payload。
- Roadmap / 路线图已将详细进程管理、进程表、详情和 guarded actions 标记为部分完成 MVP。

### 操作保护

- `SignalProcess` 拒绝非法 PID。
- PID 1 禁止操作。
- kernel thread 禁止操作。
- `expectedCommand` 不匹配时拒绝发送信号。
- SIGTERM / SIGKILL 都需要 UI 二次确认。
- 进程不存在或已退出时显示中文错误，不静默失败。

### 安全 / 架构

- 未修改 SSH runtime。
- 未修改 SSH Terminal PTY 生命周期。
- 未重写 Monitor runtime。
- 未修改 SFTP/SCP runtime。
- 未修改 Tunnel runtime。
- 未修改 Docker manager runtime。
- 未修改 Local Terminal runtime。
- 未修改 workspace state model。
- 未修改 SecretStore / Key Vault。
- 未修改 Backup / Import / Export secret 行为。
- Process Manager 是 Monitor attachment layer，使用独立 SSH exec。
- process runtime state 不进入 backup/export。
- 不读取 `/proc/[pid]/environ`；详情返回 `environmentRedacted=true`。
- 完整 cmdline 不写入日志。
- 不记录进程输出、terminal output、环境变量、secret、Docker logs 或文件内容。

- `go vet ./...`：通过。
- `go test ./...`：通过。

  既有 jsdom canvas `getContext` warning 不影响结果。
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`（frontend）：通过，保留既有 Vite chunk-size warning。

## 2026-06-19: Docker Stats Layout and Batch Action Stabilization

Baseline commit:
`73a66c661efffba1f6c8306a06efb8738ac52d01`

Stage goal:
Fix real-user Docker / container management regressions around Stats refresh
layout drift, mixed-selection batch actions, unavailable batch stop, and missing
pending feedback for slow container operations.

### Root Causes

- Left-side container cards conditionally inserted CPU/MEM stat spans only after
  stats data existed. Refreshing the right-side Stats panel changed the card
  child structure and could shift/overlap the compact list text.
- Batch actions used all-selected matching rules in the UI, so one selected
  container already in the target/inapplicable state could disable or block the
  whole batch.
- Backend batch start/stop/restart did not classify ineligible containers as
  skipped results.
- Single and batch container operations only used a generic busy flag, so slow
  stop/start/restart/delete looked like no action had happened.

### Implemented

- Left-side Docker cards now always render fixed stat placeholders:
  - `CPU —`;
  - `MEM —`.
- Stats refresh updates only the stat values and no longer inserts/removes
  left-card stat nodes.
- Card grid reserves stable columns for checkbox, main identity, meta/stats,
  and actions.
- Batch start/stop/restart/delete now calculate an eligible subset on the
  frontend and call the backend only with eligible container IDs.
- If no selected container is eligible, the UI shows a Chinese toast instead of
  silently doing nothing.
- Batch delete confirmation now says how many stopped containers will be
  deleted and how many running/ineligible containers will be skipped.
- Backend batch responses now include:
  - `status`;
  - `reason`;
  - `skippedCount`.
- Backend batch start:
  - starts exited/dead containers;
  - skips running or otherwise ineligible containers.
- Backend batch stop:
  - stops running/restarting containers;
  - skips already stopped containers;
  - continues after individual Docker command failure.
- Backend batch restart:
  - restarts running containers;
  - skips stopped/exited containers.
- Backend batch remove:
  - removes stopped/dead containers;
  - skips running/paused/restarting containers;
  - still never uses `docker rm -f`.
- Added per-container pending action state:
  - `启动中...`;
  - `停止中...`;
  - `重启中...`;
  - `删除中...`.
- Added batch pending summary such as:
  `正在停止 N 个容器...`.
- Pending operations disable conflicting duplicate clicks while keeping the
  dialog scrollable.
- Batch toasts now report success, failure, and skipped counts.
- Operation completion still refreshes the container list and prunes missing
  selections.

### Safety / Architecture

- No SSH runtime change.
- No Terminal PTY lifecycle change.
- No Monitor runtime change.
- No SFTP/SCP runtime change.
- No Local Terminal runtime change.
- No Tunnel runtime change.
- No `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantic change.
- No workspace state model change.
- No SecretStore / Key Vault change.
- No Backup / Import / Export secret behavior change.
- Docker manager remains an independent SSH exec subsystem.
- Docker logs, Env, full inspect output, secrets, terminal IO, passwords,
  passphrases, private keys, and SecretStore values are not logged.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 43 files and
  332 tests. Existing jsdom canvas warning remains non-fatal.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`F1CCC6C39EAFD100E57792208B725D31FBDBAF7C191D1EA4E73D328F64586F4A`

### Manual Verification Checklist

1. Open `容器管理`.
2. Click a container's Stats panel.
3. Click `刷新 Stats`.
4. Confirm the left-side container list text no longer shifts, overlaps, or
   duplicates.
5. Confirm left-side card CPU/MEM positions remain fixed before and after
   stats data appears.
6. Select running and stopped containers together.
7. Click batch start and confirm only stopped/exited containers are started,
   while running containers are skipped.
8. Click batch stop and confirm only running/restarting containers are stopped,
   while stopped containers are skipped.
9. Click batch restart and confirm only running containers are restarted.
10. Batch delete running + stopped containers and confirm only stopped/dead
    containers are deleted or submitted for delete; running containers are
    skipped.
11. Stop one running container and confirm the card immediately shows
    `停止中...`.
12. During slow stop, confirm the UI still scrolls and does not look frozen.
13. During batch stop, confirm the batch bar shows
    `正在停止 N 个容器...`.
14. Confirm final toast reports success/failure/skipped counts.
15. Confirm the container list refreshes after operations.
16. Confirm missing containers are removed from selection after refresh.
17. Confirm SSH/SFTP/SCP/Monitor/Tunnel/Local Terminal remain unaffected.

### Remaining Risks / Not Done

- Current Docker batch/stats/pending fix still needs user real-machine
  verification against an actual Docker host.
- Batch operations remain sequential Docker CLI commands through the Docker
  manager; no per-container progress stream beyond pending labels and final
  summary toast.
- Docker Compose, Kubernetes, registry login, `docker run`, interactive
  `docker exec`, and `docker system prune` remain out of scope.

## 2026-06-19: Docker Compact Container List and Batch Actions

Baseline commit:
`55aaaa2ab35fd532875df51642790daa3cf8b282`

Stage goal:
Optimize Docker / container management UI density and add multi-select batch
operations without changing any frozen runtime architecture.

### Root Causes

- Container cards used a two-row action-heavy layout; the per-card
  `日志` / `Stats` / `信息` buttons duplicated the right-side detail tabs and made
  the list too tall.
- Stats and Info detail content inherited a loose panel layout and could appear
  lower than expected instead of starting at the top.
- The Docker UI had no selection state, so users had to start/stop/restart/delete
  containers one by one.

### Implemented

- Docker container cards now use a compact grid:
  - checkbox at the far left;
  - name/image/short ID in the main column;
  - status/ports and CPU/MEM in compact columns;
  - only runtime actions on the right.
- Left-side cards no longer show `日志`, `Stats`, or `信息` buttons.
- Right-side `日志` / `Stats` / `信息` tabs remain the only detail view.
- Stats and Info panels are wrapped in a top-aligned detail content area.
- Added per-container checkbox selection.
- Added select-all for the current filtered results.
- Added selected count in the list header.
- Added batch action bar when selected count is greater than zero:
  - start;
  - stop;
  - restart;
  - delete.
- Checkbox clicks only toggle selection and do not switch the right-side detail
  container.
- Card body clicks still switch the right-side detail container.
- Selection state is isolated by `serverID`.
- Refresh and Docker container events prune selections for containers that no
  longer exist.
- Added typed backend batch APIs:
  - `DockerBatchStartContainers`;
  - `DockerBatchStopContainers`;
  - `DockerBatchRestartContainers`;
  - `DockerBatchRemoveContainers`.
- Batch APIs return per-container results plus success/failure counts.
- Batch command failure for one container does not abort the rest of the batch.
- Batch delete still inspects each container and refuses running, paused, or
  restarting containers.
- No `docker rm -f` was added.
- Batch delete uses the existing danger confirmation dialog.
- Batch operation toasts report success and failure counts in Chinese.

### Safety / Architecture

- No SSH runtime change.
- No Terminal PTY lifecycle change.
- No Monitor runtime change.
- No SFTP/SCP runtime change.
- No Local Terminal runtime change.
- No Tunnel runtime change.
- No `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantic change.
- No workspace state model change.
- No SecretStore / Key Vault change.
- No Backup / Import / Export secret behavior change.
- Docker manager remains an independent SSH exec subsystem.
- Docker logs, Env, full inspect output, secrets, terminal IO, passwords,
  passphrases, private keys, and SecretStore values are not logged.

## 2026-06-18: Tunnel Duplicate Name, Layout, Delete Verification, and GatewayPorts Restart Fix

Baseline commit:
`30728d3516acc52be37c30401ea3fb30ed4b0543`

Stage goal:
Fix real-user tunnel issues around duplicate profile names, compact profile
cards, edit/list placement, GatewayPorts applying only after tunnel restart, and
profile deletion leaving ports reachable.

### Root Causes

- SQLite surfaced the raw unique index error for duplicate tunnel profile names
  because profile save did not preflight `server_id + name` conflicts and did
  not map the SQLite fallback error.
- Deleting a profile could remove UI/config state before proving the associated
  runtime listener had actually stopped and released its port.
- Reloading `sshd` after changing GatewayPorts does not mutate an already-open
  remote-forward listener; the old listener must be stopped and recreated.
- Profile cards kept their actions below the diagnostics, making every card
  taller than needed.

### Implemented

- `SaveTunnelProfile` now checks duplicate names per server before insert/update.
- Duplicate profile names return typed error
  `TUNNEL_PROFILE_NAME_EXISTS: 该服务器下已存在同名端口转发配置，请修改名称。`.
- Update excludes the current profile ID, so saving an unchanged profile name no
  longer produces a false duplicate.
- SQLite unique-constraint fallback for `idx_tunnel_profiles_server_name` maps
  to the same Chinese error and does not expose the raw index or `(2067)`.
- `StopTunnel` and `StopProfile` now return release-verification errors.
- Runtime stop closes local/remote/SOCKS listeners, active connections, accept
  loop context, waits for worker completion, then verifies port release.
- Local and dynamic tunnels verify release by checking that local TCP connect
  fails.
- Remote tunnels verify release through the existing tunnel SSH transport with
  `ss/netstat`; missing tools produce a clear Chinese failure instead of a fake
  success.
- `DeleteTunnelProfile` now fails before deleting the SQLite profile if runtime
  stop or release verification fails.
- Added a typed `EnableRemoteForwardAccessAndRestart` API.
- GatewayPorts flow is now:
  stop current remote listener -> verify port release -> modify only
  `GatewayPorts yes` -> `sshd -t` -> reload sshd -> restart tunnel -> recheck
  remote listener.
- The helper still does not force-modify `AllowTcpForwarding` and still rejects
  unsupported Dropbear/unknown cases.
- Tunnel UI maps raw unique constraint errors to the Chinese duplicate-name
  toast.
- Profile cards are more compact; start/stop/recheck/delete actions are in the
  top-right header.
- Edit form is pinned to the left column and created profile list to the right
  column on normal window sizes.
- Loopback-only remote cards now show `放行并重启` instead of the old one-step
  `启用远程访问`.
- The old tunnel runtime is removed from store state when the restart flow
  returns a new runtime.
- Delete failure keeps the profile visible in UI.

### Safety / Architecture

- No SSH terminal runtime change.
- No Terminal PTY lifecycle change.
- No Monitor runtime change.
- No SFTP/SCP runtime change.
- No Local Terminal runtime change.
- No `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantic change.
- No workspace state model change.
- No SecretStore / Key Vault change.
- No Backup / Import / Export change.
- Tunnel runtime remains independent from terminal/SFTP/monitor sessions.
- No tunnel payload, passwords, passphrases, private keys, terminal IO, or full
  sshd config content is logged.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 41 files and
  306 tests. Vitest still prints the existing jsdom canvas warning.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed after cleaning generated Wails whitespace.

`D5FBF33CF3F5D6EE858B3F3A9043F5A6407B44BCB21001AA7774E790619D74FE`

7. Confirm the flow first stops the tunnel and releases the remote port.
8. Confirm GatewayPorts yes is written, `sshd -t` passes, and sshd reloads.
9. Confirm the tunnel automatically restarts and remote listen is rechecked.
10. If recheck becomes public, confirm old warning/help/button disappear.
11. Delete running local tunnel and confirm the local port is released.
12. Delete running remote tunnel and confirm the server port is no longer
    listening.
13. Delete running SOCKS tunnel and confirm the local port is released.
14. Confirm SSH/SFTP/SCP/Monitor/Local Terminal are unaffected.

### Remaining Risks / Not Done

- Firewall and LAN reachability still require real server/network verification.
- Dropbear/OpenWrt GatewayPorts editing remains unsupported.
- No interactive sudo password prompt is implemented.
- If the remote server lacks both `ss` and `netstat`, ServerPilot reports that
  release verification cannot be proven and does not delete the profile.

## 2026-06-18: Tunnel UX Simplification and Lifecycle Hardening Follow-up

Baseline commit:
`50cc3d9a6a272257ebdf50c2ec978891ebe608a1`

Stage goal:
Continue simplifying the port forwarding UI and close the remaining UX gaps
around GatewayPorts recheck state, profile-card editing, unsaved edits, and
running-profile deletion.

### Implemented

- Top toolbar now has only:
  - current server selector;
  - `新建端口转发`.
- The server selector no longer stretches across the whole toolbar, and the
  create button aligns vertically with it.
- Form type choices are now concise:
  - `本地转发`;
  - `远程转发`;
  - `SOCKS5 代理`.
- Descriptions moved below/inside the selected type area instead of using long
  card titles.
- SOCKS5 text explicitly says it creates a local SOCKS5 proxy through the SSH
  server.
- Removed the `高级选项` collapsible section.
- `名称` is now a normal top-level field.
- `连接服务器后自动启动` is now a normal checkbox, not hidden as an advanced
  option.
- Removed the `编辑` button from profile cards.
- Clicking a profile card now enters edit mode.
- Stop/delete/recheck/start buttons use click propagation guards so they do not
  also select/edit the card.
- The active edited card is highlighted.
- Added unsaved-edit protection when switching from one profile card to another:
  - `保存` saves the current form first, then switches;
  - `放弃` discards current form changes, then switches;
  - `取消` stays on the current edit.
- Edit mode keeps the original profile ID through type changes; saving still
  calls `UpdateTunnelProfile`, not create.
- Running profile delete now explicitly stops the active runtime in the UI
  before delete, while backend `DeleteTunnelProfile` still calls `StopProfile`
  as the lifecycle guarantee for all runtimes bound to that profile.
- GatewayPorts helper UI now treats `remoteListenExposure=public` as authoritative:
  - hides old loopback-only warning;
  - hides GatewayPorts help;
  - hides `启用远程访问`;
  - shows `运行中（局域网可访问）`.
- GatewayPorts text now says usually only `GatewayPorts yes` is needed; some
  servers may also need AllowTcpForwarding. The helper still only modifies
  GatewayPorts and does not force-modify AllowTcpForwarding.

### Safety / Architecture

- No SSH terminal runtime change.
- No Terminal PTY lifecycle change.
- No Monitor runtime change.
- No SFTP/SCP runtime change.
- No Local Terminal runtime change.
- No `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantic change.
- No workspace state model change.
- No SecretStore / Key Vault change.
- No Backup / Import / Export change.
- Tunnel runtime remains independent from terminal/SFTP/monitor sessions.

## 2026-06-18: Tunnel Lifecycle, Edit Flow, GatewayPorts Helper, and Config UX

Baseline commit:
`bb4526762324c95c8769397d9a185db4eb1acd85`

Stage goal:
Fix tunnel profile deletion/editing lifecycle bugs, provide an explicit dangerous
GatewayPorts enable flow for loopback-only remote forwards, and simplify the
tunnel configuration panel into user-facing scenarios.

### Root Causes

- `DeleteTunnelProfile` removed the stored profile but did not first stop all
  runtime workers for that profile, so local/remote/SOCKS listeners could remain
  reachable after the UI record disappeared.
- The edit form reset create/edit state when the tunnel type changed, so editing
  an existing profile could call create-style behavior instead of updating the
  original profile.
- Remote loopback-only diagnostics only explained GatewayPorts; there was no
  guarded helper to perform the dangerous server-side change.
- The tunnel form exposed SSH forwarding terms directly, making common scenarios
  harder to understand.

### Implemented

- `DeleteTunnelProfile(id)` now calls tunnel manager `StopProfile(id)` before
  deleting the profile from SQLite.
- `StopProfile(profileID)` stops every local, remote, or dynamic runtime worker
  bound to the profile and waits for listener shutdown through the existing
  worker stop path.
- Tunnel store tombstones deleted profile IDs, removes their runtimes from UI
  state, and ignores late state events for those profile IDs.
- Editing keeps the original `profileID` even when changing type. Saving calls
  `UpdateTunnelProfile`, not `CreateTunnelProfile`.
- Running profile edits require confirmation. After confirmation, ServerPilot
  stops the old runtime, saves the updated profile, and restarts the updated
  tunnel.
- Remote forwarding defaults in the simplified form now use `0.0.0.0:12380`
  for the server-open side, matching the GatewayPorts workflow.
- Added typed APIs:
  - `InspectRemoteForwardAccess`;
  - `EnableRemoteForwardAccess`.
- Loopback-only remote cards now show `启用远程访问`. Clicking it opens a danger
  confirmation and then runs the GatewayPorts helper.
- The helper:
  - supports OpenSSH only;
  - rejects Dropbear/unknown unsupported cases;
  - requires root or non-interactive `sudo -n`;
  - backs up `sshd_config` to `*.serverpilot.bak.<timestamp>`;
  - writes or replaces only `GatewayPorts yes`;
  - does not force `AllowTcpForwarding`;
  - inserts before the first `Match` block when no global GatewayPorts entry
    exists;
  - validates with `sshd -t`;
  - rolls back from backup if validation fails;
  - attempts sshd reload with common systemctl/service/init.d commands;
  - returns a warning if reload fails after validation succeeds.
- Simplified the config panel into three scenario cards:
  - `访问服务器上的服务` for local forward;
  - `让服务器访问我电脑上的服务` for remote forward;
  - `SOCKS5 代理` for dynamic forwarding.
- Moved less common fields such as name and auto-start into `高级选项`.

### Safety / Architecture

- Tunnel runtime remains an independent SSH subsystem.
- No terminal, monitor, SFTP/SCP, or local terminal runtime changes.
- No `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantic change.
- No workspace state model change.
- No SecretStore, Key Vault, Backup, Import, or Export change.
- GatewayPorts helper logs only lifecycle markers/results, not sshd config
  contents, tunnel traffic, secrets, terminal input/output, passwords, keys, or
  passphrases.
- The helper never silently modifies remote config; it requires explicit user
  confirmation from the UI.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 41 files and 299 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`8BFE4A23DC929AE040488BEE77B8EF28AC1860C824C2D6E8F4AD3ADC35F43B3A`

1. Create remote tunnel `0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`.
2. If actual listen remains `127.0.0.1:12380` / `[::1]:12380`, UI must show
   `运行中（仅服务器本机）` and an `启用远程访问` button.
3. Click `启用远程访问`; danger confirmation must appear before any server config
   change.
4. After confirmation, verify the server creates an sshd_config backup and adds
   `GatewayPorts yes`.
5. Confirm `AllowTcpForwarding` was not force-modified.
6. Confirm `sshd -t` passes before reload; if validation fails, config must be
   restored from backup.
7. Recheck tunnel listener after reload; public listen should update to
   `0.0.0.0` / LAN-accessible when sshd and firewall allow it.
8. Delete a running local, remote, or dynamic tunnel profile; the port must be
   released and later events must not revive the deleted runtime in UI.
9. Edit an existing profile, change type, save, and confirm the profile is
   updated in place without duplicate profiles.
10. SSH/SFTP/SCP/Monitor/Local Terminal must remain unaffected.

### Remaining Risks / Not Done

- Dropbear/OpenWrt GatewayPorts editing is intentionally unsupported in this
  round.
- The helper does not collect sudo passwords; non-root users need passwordless
  sudo or must change sshd config manually.
- Firewall rules are still outside ServerPilot and require real server/network
  verification.

## 2026-06-18: Remote Tunnel Loopback-Only Classification

Baseline commit:
`a12123aae317d865133426c569fa7e51ebc49eaa`

Stage goal:
Fix the UI ambiguity when remote forwarding is alive but sshd only binds the
remote listener to server loopback addresses.

### Real User Evidence

Remote forward:
`0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`

Server-side check:
`ss -ltn | grep 12380`

Observed:

```text
LISTEN 0 128 127.0.0.1:12380 0.0.0.0:*
LISTEN 0 128 [::1]:12380 [::]:*
```

Conclusion: remote listener lifecycle is correct, but sshd only exposes the
listener on loopback. External access to `192.168.0.88:12380` will be refused.

### Implemented

- Added `remoteListenExposure`:
  - `public`;
  - `loopback_only`;
  - `not_listening`;
  - `unknown`.
- Added `effectiveListenAddrs` to preserve all detected listening addresses.
- Correctly parses:
  - `127.0.0.1:PORT` as `loopback_only`;
  - `[::1]:PORT` as `loopback_only`;
  - `0.0.0.0:PORT` as `public`;
  - `[::]:PORT` as `public`;
  - specific non-loopback server IP as `public`.
- If the same port has both loopback and public listeners, exposure is
  `public`.
- UI status now shows:
  - `运行中（仅服务器本机）` for loopback-only listeners;
  - `运行中（局域网可访问）` for public listeners;
  - `运行中（未确认监听）` for unknown detection.
- Loopback-only status uses warning styling, not normal green running.
- Loopback-only cards show GatewayPorts guidance:
  `AllowTcpForwarding yes`, `GatewayPorts yes`, or
  `GatewayPorts clientspecified`, plus sshd restart and firewall reminder.
- Test commands now follow actual exposure:
  - loopback SSH: `ssh -p PORT root@127.0.0.1`;
  - public SSH: `ssh -p PORT root@serverIP`;
  - loopback HTTP: `curl http://127.0.0.1:PORT`;
  - public HTTP: `curl http://serverIP:PORT`.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 41 files and 291 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`56CCEFF1E7CDA4DC1B926CBF128338B1E7B00EB217EF9D249D989AF59896EFE5`

   `0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`.

   `ss -ltn | grep 12380`.
3. If result contains:
   - `127.0.0.1:12380`;
   - `[::1]:12380`;
   UI must show `运行中（仅服务器本机）`.

6. If Windows external access to `192.168.0.88:12380` is refused, UI must not

## 2026-06-18: Remote Tunnel Listener Verification

Baseline commit:
`b824133d698a877bdfd74cc68dd429afc0f86141`

Stage goal:
Fix the remote port forwarding state bug where UI showed `运行中` even though
external access to the remote forwarded port returned `connection refused`.

### Root Cause

- Remote forwarding treated `ssh.Client.Listen("tcp", bind)` success as enough
  evidence to show plain running.
- There was no post-start diagnostic to verify the server actually had a TCP
  listener for the requested remote port.
- If sshd downgraded `0.0.0.0` to loopback because of `GatewayPorts`, the UI did
  not expose the effective bind address.
- The listener lifecycle itself is now covered by tests: it remains alive after
  `StartTunnel` returns and is only closed by `StopTunnel` / server lifecycle
  shutdown.

### Implemented

- Added typed remote listen diagnostics to `TunnelRuntime`:
  - requested listen address;
  - actual listen address;
  - effective remote bind host;
  - `unchecked/listening/loopback_only/not_listening/unknown` status;
  - warning text;
  - protocol-aware test command.
- Added remote-side detection through the tunnel SSH client:
  - `ss -ltn` first;
  - fallback to `netstat -ltn`;
  - command-missing result becomes `unknown`, not a crash.
- Remote tunnel startup now runs listen verification after `ListenTCP`.
- If the requested remote port is not detected, startup fails, resources are
  closed, and UI cannot show ordinary `running`.
- If requested bind is `0.0.0.0` but actual bind is loopback, the tunnel stays
  running with a visible GatewayPorts warning.
- Added `CheckTunnelRemoteListen` typed Wails API and a UI `重新检测` action.
- Remote tunnel cards now show:
  - requested listen;
  - actual listen;
  - listen check status;
  - GatewayPorts/unknown/not-listening warning;
  - SSH/curl/nc test command based on target port.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 41 files and 291 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`AFBC6887B06C419D29183BF2FDBCF961447F3DD07D436E154BBB16CF23EFA56C`

### Manual Verification Checklist

1. Create remote forwarding:
   `0.0.0.0:12380 -> 192.168.0.252:22`.
2. Start the tunnel and confirm the card shows requested listen
   `0.0.0.0:12380`.
3. Confirm the card shows actual listen:
   - `0.0.0.0:12380` / `[::]:12380` when externally reachable;
   - `127.0.0.1:12380` when sshd only allows loopback;
   - `未检测到` when the remote listener is absent;
   - `未知` when `ss` and `netstat` are unavailable.
4. On server `192.168.0.88`, run:
   `ss -ltn | grep 12380`.
5. If actual listen is loopback, test inside server SSH terminal:
   `ssh -p 12380 root@127.0.0.1`.
6. If actual listen is wildcard/public, test from Windows:
   `ssh -p 12380 root@192.168.0.88`.
7. Click `重新检测` and confirm actual listen / warning / test command update.
8. Stop the tunnel and confirm the remote port is released.
9. Confirm SSH terminal, SFTP/SCP, Monitor, Local Terminal, SecretStore, Key
   Vault, Backup / Export are unchanged.

### Remaining Risks / Not Done

- This verifies the remote server listener, but it cannot prove external
  firewall reachability from every client network.
- If both `ss` and `netstat` are unavailable, the UI shows `unknown` instead of
  failing the tunnel.
- No tunnel auto-start behavior was added.

## 2026-06-18: Direct Windows ConPTY Adapter Implemented But Still Gated

Baseline commit:
`25a0072d6e4dce4117014d4f2c00136ecb74a891`

Stage goal:
Implement a real Windows Direct ConPTY adapter and enable Local Terminal only if
PowerShell and cmd pass real enabled GUI smoke. Result: Direct ConPTY adapter is
implemented, but Local Terminal remains default-disabled because the full
release conditions were not met.

### Root Cause

- The old Windows path depended on the third-party PTY wrapper from the manager
  layer and did not provide a project-owned Direct ConPTY adapter.
- Direct ConPTY itself now works for cmd in a backend smoke test.
- However, `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` empirically breaks ConPTY input/output on this
  Windows runtime:
  - go-pty with no extra flags: cmd echo smoke works;
  - go-pty with `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`: cmd echo smoke works;
  - go-pty with `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`: cmd echo smoke times out;
  - go-pty with both flags: cmd echo smoke times out.
- Because the task explicitly required `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, and enabled
  PowerShell/cmd GUI smoke was not executed and passed, Local Terminal cannot be
  safely exposed.

### Implemented

- Added a Windows-only Direct ConPTY adapter in `internal/localterminal`.
- Replaced the Windows runtime path with:
  - `CreatePseudoConsole`;
  - `ResizePseudoConsole`;
  - `ClosePseudoConsole`;
  - `STARTUPINFOEX`;
  - `PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_PSEUDOCONSOLE`;
  - direct `InitializeProcThreadAttributeList`;
  - direct `UpdateProcThreadAttribute`;
  - direct `DeleteProcThreadAttributeList`;
  - `CreateProcessW`;
  - `EXTENDED_STARTUPINFO_PRESENT`;
  - `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`;
  - UTF-16 command line;
  - stdin/stdout pipes.
- Kept Local Terminal release gate closed.
- `OpenLocalTerminal` still returns `LOCAL_TERMINAL_DISABLED`.
- `+` menu and Settings Local Terminal shell preference remain hidden.
- Added lifecycle diagnostics for Local Terminal open/exit/close with only
  sessionID, shell basename, PID, state, and exit code. Input/output, cwd, and
  user commands are not logged.
- Non-Windows platform PTY creation moved behind the same `startPlatformPTY`
  boundary without touching SSH terminal runtime.

### Direct ConPTY Test Result

- `TestDirectConPTYCmdEchoSmoke` passed.
- Test creates Direct ConPTY, starts `cmd.exe`, resizes the pseudoconsole,
  writes `echo serverpilot-conpty-unit-smoke`, reads the marker from the output
  pipe, sends `exit`, and waits for process exit.
- `GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent`, `CTRL_C_EVENT`, and `CTRL_BREAK_EVENT` remain
  absent from the adapter.

### Windows GUI Smoke

- Default-disabled smoke was executed against `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`.
- App launched with a real main window.
- Main window was closed via `WM_CLOSE`, not process kill.
- Result: process exited normally.
- Child processes before close: one expected `msedgewebview2.exe`.
- Child processes after close: 0.
- Shell child processes after close: 0.
- New residual `ServerPilot.exe` processes after close: 0.
- Enabled PowerShell GUI smoke was not executed.
- Enabled cmd GUI smoke was not executed.
- Reason: the release conditions were not met because `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` breaks
  ConPTY I/O in the reproducible backend comparison.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go test ./internal/localterminal -count=1 -v -timeout=60s`: passed,
  including `TestDirectConPTYCmdEchoSmoke`.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and 274 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`403939C30DF232DF564139160E4CA08B0CEF937F11624FB5FAE849CC65DAEE8C`

1. Open the `+` server menu and confirm Local Terminal is still absent.
2. Open Settings and confirm Local Terminal shell preference is still hidden.
3. Close the app normally and confirm no residual ServerPilot process remains.
4. Confirm no `cmd.exe`, `powershell.exe`, or `pwsh.exe` child process is left

## 2026-06-18: Keep Local Terminal Quarantined Until ConPTY Smoke Passes

Baseline commit:

Stage goal:
Re-check the Windows Local Terminal path and either prove a stable embedded
ConPTY implementation or keep the feature quarantined. The result is the safe
quarantine path: Local Terminal remains default-disabled.

### Root Cause

- The existing Windows Local Terminal runtime still uses the third-party PTY
  wrapper path, with Windows process flags added on top.
- That is not the direct, controlled ConPTY adapter required for release:
  `CreatePseudoConsole`, `ResizePseudoConsole`, `ClosePseudoConsole`,
  `STARTUPINFOEX`, pseudo-console attribute wiring, and owned pipe lifecycle.
- Because the enabled path has not passed real Windows GUI smoke for
  PowerShell and cmd, exposing the entry would recreate the previous risk:
  blank terminal, no input, external shell windows, or shell/tab close affecting
  the app process.

### Completed

- Added a hard release gate in `internal/localterminal` so the old
  `SERVERPILOT_LOCAL_TERMINAL_EXPERIMENTAL=1` flag alone cannot expose the
  unstable Local Terminal runtime.
- `OpenLocalTerminal` still returns `LOCAL_TERMINAL_DISABLED` while the release
  gate is closed.
- Disabled `ListLocalTerminals` now returns an empty slice instead of nil.
- Added App-level tests proving disabled Open/Write/Resize/GetState do not enter
  the Local Terminal runtime or start a PTY factory.
- Kept `CloseLocalTerminal` safe and idempotent while disabled.
- Local Terminal Settings remains hidden while disabled.
- The `+` menu Local Terminal entry remains hidden while disabled.
- Settings capability loading now tolerates `shellOptions: null` defensively.

## 2026-06-18: Append New Terminal Tabs To The Right

Baseline commit:
`fd6a7aa71b21ab8af98d23bcf27148138b0eabd1`

Stage goal:
Fix the UI tab ordering regression where newly opened server/terminal tabs
could appear near the left because topbar rendering grouped terminals by
`workspaceOrder`.

### Completed

- `WorkspaceTabs.vue` now renders terminal tabs in `store.tabs` global order.
- New server terminal tabs append to the far right.
- New same-server terminal tabs append to the far right instead of being grouped
  beside the same server's older tab.
- Per-server display labels still use server-local indexes, e.g. `server #2`.
- Workspace-only tabs are still rendered for workspaces without terminal
  sessions, after active terminal tabs.
- Existing close behavior is preserved:
  - non-final terminal tab close stays session-scoped;
  - final terminal tab close still triggers server-wide disconnect through the
    existing final-disconnect event path.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Root Cause

- The topbar displayed terminals by iterating `workspaceOrder` first and then
  rendering all terminal sessions for each server.
- That made `A | B` become `A | A #2 | B` when creating another terminal for
  server A.
- The intended behavior is append order: `A | B | A #2`.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Added WorkspaceTabs coverage proving a new same-server terminal is appended
  right after existing tabs.
- Added WorkspaceTabs coverage proving a newly opened server terminal is
  appended at the far right.
- Existing WorkspaceTabs close/lifecycle tests continue to pass.

## 2026-06-18: Final Terminal Tab Server Disconnect Lifecycle Fix

Baseline commit:
`897ddb468b50d38b3310a6ccc6e6eea41e267d3c`

Stage goal:
Fix the serious lifecycle bug where closing all visible server/terminal tabs
left the server runtime online, the `+` server menu green, and monitor data
still viewable. This is a lifecycle hookup fix only.

### Completed

- Final terminal tab close now means server-wide disconnect for that `serverID`.
- Top tab close and terminal-workspace close both use the same decision:
  - if the same server still has other terminal tabs, close only the current
    terminal session and its bound SFTP context;
  - if this is the server's final terminal tab, remove the UI tab/workspace
    immediately and trigger `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Final-tab close no longer calls `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` directly. The
  backend server-wide lifecycle owns terminal/monitor/SFTP/SCP/transfer cleanup.
- App-level final disconnect now immediately:
  - clears frontend SFTP/SCP state and transfers for the server;
  - marks frontend connection/monitor state disconnected;
  - suppresses late monitor/connection events through the existing server
    store guard;
  - then calls the existing `DisconnectServer(serverID)` API.
- SFTP frontend store now suppresses late SFTP entries/state/error/transfer
  events after `clearServer(serverID)` until that server is explicitly opened
  again.
- `sftpStore.clearServer(serverID)` now also removes stale
  `stateByServerId[serverID]`, so SFTP cannot keep showing an old online state.
- Other server IDs are unaffected by a final-tab disconnect.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Root Cause

- The previous fix removed the last terminal tab UI shell, but it kept using the
  terminal-session close path (`CloseTerminal` + `CloseSftpContext`).
- That bypassed the server-wide lifecycle coordinator, so Monitor and other
  server runtime state could remain active.
- The `+` menu status comes from frontend connection state, and without
  `store.markDisconnected(serverID)` it could keep showing the stale online
  state from monitor/runtime events.
- SFTP frontend state also kept `stateByServerId[serverID]`, so the bottom file
  panel could still see old online context state after a visual tab close.

### Tests Added / Updated

- WorkspaceTabs tests:
  - non-final terminal close still calls only `CloseTerminal` and the bound
    `CloseSftpContext`;
  - final tab close emits server final-disconnect and does not call
    `CloseTerminal`;
  - context-menu "close current terminal" on the final tab uses the same
    server-disconnect path;
  - "close all terminal sessions for this server" disconnects that server;
  - sibling server tabs remain visible and active.
- TerminalWorkspace tests:
  - closing the final terminal from the workspace emits server final-disconnect
    and removes the workspace shell.
- App navigation tests:
  - final-terminal disconnect calls backend `DisconnectServer(serverID)`;
  - `+` menu status changes from online to disconnected for that server;
  - monitor snapshot/history are cleared;
  - SFTP state and transfers for the server are cleared;
  - a second server remains online;
  - late monitor/connection events do not make the closed server green again.
- SFTP store tests:
  - `clearServer` removes stale server-level online state;
  - late SFTP state/list/transfer events after clear are ignored;
  - a later explicit SFTP open resumes event acceptance.

### Validation

- `gofmt -w (git ls-files '*.go')`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and
  272 tests. The existing jsdom canvas warning remains non-fatal.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed after cleaning Wails-generated trailing whitespace.

## 2026-06-18: Per-Server Final Terminal Tab Close Fix

Baseline commit:
`22288dcc2caf01cac165af79c878613019433098`

Stage goal:
Fix the remaining real-machine regression where each server's final terminal
tab needed two close attempts. This is a terminal tab UI/store cleanup fix only.

### Completed

- Closing a terminal tab now removes that terminal tab immediately.
- When the closed tab is the last terminal for its `serverID`, the matching
  workspace shell is removed from `workspaces`, `workspaceOrder`, and workspace
  history in the same action.
- The close path no longer converts the final tab into a disconnected/offline
  workspace tab that requires a second click.
- Closing a terminal tab still calls only `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` plus the
  terminal-bound `CloseSftpContext`.
- Closing a terminal tab does not call `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Explicit `disconnectServer(connectionId, false)` still preserves the existing
  disconnected workspace behavior because the workspace-shell removal is scoped
  to `closeSession(sessionID)`.
- Closed-session tombstones still suppress late terminal output/status events,
  so late events cannot recreate a closed terminal tab.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Root Cause

- `closeSession(sessionID)` removed the terminal session but, when that session
  was the last terminal for the server, it only marked the server workspace as
  `disconnected`.
- `WorkspaceTabs.vue` intentionally renders workspace-only entries from
  `workspaceOrder`, so the removed terminal was replaced by an offline
  workspace shell.
- The second click closed that workspace shell, which made the close operation
  feel like it required two clicks per server.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Store tests now assert:
  - final terminal close removes the workspace shell;
  - per-server final terminal close removes only that server's shell while other
    server terminals remain;
  - inactive server final terminal close does not switch away from the active
    terminal;
  - late status events for a closed session do not revive the removed tab or
    workspace.
- Workspace tab tests now assert:
  - close button on a final terminal does not leave a disconnected workspace
    tab;
  - another server's final terminal closes once without touching sibling server
    tabs;
  - context-menu "close current terminal" uses the same one-action behavior;
  - `DisconnectServer` is still emitted only by the explicit disconnect action.

## 2026-06-18: 4-Item Regression Polish

Baseline commit:
`e8a14bffa32d2bcd44095b0b024d7e7335d824d7`

Stage goal:
Fix four real-machine regressions without changing frozen SSH, Terminal,
Monitor, SFTP, workspace, DisconnectServer, SecretStore, Key Vault, Backup, or
Local Terminal architecture.

### Completed

- Terminal tab close:
  - closing the final terminal tab now clears `activeSessionId` and
    `activeWorkspaceServerId` in one action;
  - late terminal status/output events remain tombstoned and cannot revive the
    closed tab;
  - one-terminal close still calls only `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` and the
    matching `CloseSftpContext`;
  - it does not call `DisconnectServer(serverID)` and does not stop Monitor.
- SCP default path:
  - SCP fallback now resolves a real absolute home path through shell:
    `cd ~ && pwd -P`;
  - if home cannot be resolved, the remote script falls back to enterable
    `/root`, then `/`;
  - SCP `Home` uses the resolved home path instead of `.`;
  - legacy `.` and `./path` SCP requests are normalized to the current absolute
    directory or `/`;
  - frontend SFTP state and panel display never keep SCP `currentPath` as `.`.
- Settings overlay text:
  - Settings category labels are normal UTF-8 Chinese:
    `常规`, `终端`, `SFTP / 文件`, `命令`, `备份 / 恢复`, `密钥库`, `安全`, `高级`;
  - SFTP and Command empty category descriptions are Chinese and no longer show
    the old English placeholders.
- Settings overlay layout:
  - Settings overlay is centered in the whole app viewport;
  - it is no longer right-docked / side-drawer styled;
  - the sticky save action and existing z-index contract remain unchanged.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export secret behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go:
  - SCP fallback open resolves `/root` instead of `.`;
  - SCP fallback uses `/` when home resolution fails;
  - SCP `Home` lists the resolved absolute home path and never lists `.`;
  - SCP path normalization converts `.`, legacy dot base, and `./relative`
    inputs to safe absolute paths.
- Frontend:
  - final terminal tab closes in one click and clears active workspace state;
  - context-menu "close current terminal" also closes the last tab in one
    action;
  - final tab close still closes only the matching SFTP context;
  - SCP `currentPath: "."` is normalized/displayed as `/`;
  - Settings category labels and SFTP/Command category text are validated;
  - Settings overlay CSS is tested as centered, not side-docked.

Baseline commit:

    update path.

  - SSH terminal workspace remains mounted and visible underneath the overlay;
  - Settings content is grouped by category navigation;
  - the save action remains sticky in the overlay;
  - closing dirty Settings uses the existing app dialog flow.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export secret behavior.
- No passwords, passphrases, private keys, terminal input/output, or remote file
  contents were added to logs or docs.

  - SQLite history update returns the updated joined row;
  - SCP shell quoting covers spaces, Unicode, quotes, shell metacharacters,
    `$()`, and backticks;
  - SCP path validation rejects traversal and root delete targets;
  - SCP shell listing parser handles files, directories, symlinks, and skips

  - SCP mode manager routes list, mkdir, rename, inspect delete, delete,

    workspace;
  - command history/favorite double-click inserts without executing;
  - command history edit calls typed backend update API;

## 2026-06-18: SFTP / SCP Transfer Pause-Resume MVP

Baseline commit:
`c11a5ce6311a394f05537700ad6446ffe8d709fa`

Stage goal:
Add pause/resume controls for the existing SFTP/SCP transfer queue without
changing SSH, Terminal, Monitor, SFTP context, workspace, SecretStore, Key
Vault, Backup, or Local Terminal architecture.

### Completed

- Added typed Wails APIs:
  - `SftpPauseTransfer(request)`;
  - `SftpResumeTransfer(request)`.
- Pause/resume requests are validated by `serverID + contextID + transferID`.
- Transfer state now includes:
  - `pausing`, `paused`, and `resuming` statuses;
  - current-file byte progress;
  - `resumeOffset`;
  - `pauseRequested`, `cancelRequested`, `canPause`, `canResume`, and
    `canCancel`.
- SFTP single-file upload/download now pauses at chunk boundaries and resumes
  from the recorded offset.
- SFTP resume checks that the source/target still exists and rejects obvious
  size/mtime changes with a Chinese error instead of silently overwriting from
  the beginning.
- SFTP recursive upload/download keeps the existing plan in memory; completed
  files are not restarted after resume, and the current file keeps offset
  progress.
- SCP fallback uses cooperative pause:
  - directory transfers pause only after the current file completes;
  - resume continues the remaining files;
  - SCP does not fake arbitrary half-file offset resume.
- Paused transfers remain cancelable.
- Pause/resume does not call `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Pause/resume does not close SSH terminal sessions or SFTP contexts.
- Transfer popover now shows compact pause/resume actions beside cancel.
- Status bar shows paused transfer state and exposes a compact resume button.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- No file contents, terminal output, passwords, passphrases, private keys, or
  SecretStore contents were added to logs or docs.
- Pause/resume runtime state is not included in Backup / Export.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Go:
  - SFTP file upload pause records an offset and resumes to completion.
  - SFTP file download pause records an offset and resumes to completion.
  - Pause/resume rejects mismatched context IDs and missing transfer IDs.
  - Pausing one transfer does not mutate a sibling transfer.
  - Paused transfer can be canceled without closing the SFTP session.
- Frontend:
  - SFTP store pause/resume uses the transfer's own context.
  - Transfer popover shows pause for running transfers.
  - Transfer popover calls `SftpPauseTransfer` with `serverID/contextID/transferID`.
  - Paused transfer still shows cancel.
  - Status bar shows paused state and calls `SftpResumeTransfer`.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 39 files and 258 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

## 2026-06-18: Same-Server Terminal SFTP Context Isolation And Command Scope Filter

Baseline commit:
`8274b7df8fcd26b4a9928dc5d421cce03eda0801`

Stage goal:
Continue the terminal tab correction by fixing same-server multi-terminal SFTP
semantics and adding Command panel scope filtering. This is a targeted
interaction/runtime-boundary fix, not a new feature stage.

### Completed

- SFTP runtime state is now keyed by `serverID + contextID` instead of only
  `serverID`.
- Frontend SFTP state is now tracked by terminal-bound context:
  - `terminalSessionID -> sftpContextID`;
  - `activeSftpContextID`;
  - state, entries, selection, hidden-file toggle, sort state, upload refresh
    errors, and transfers are isolated by context.
- Same-server terminal tabs can keep different SFTP current directories.
- Switching same-server terminal tabs switches the bottom SFTP panel to that
  terminal tab's context.
- Auto-open SFTP after terminal creation now passes the new terminal
  `sessionID` as the SFTP context.
- Closing one terminal tab calls `CloseSftpContext(serverID, contextID)` and
  `CloseTerminal(sessionID)` only.
- Closing one terminal tab does not call `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- `DisconnectServer(serverID)` still closes all SFTP contexts for that server
  through the existing server lifecycle path.
- SFTP events, list results, errors, transfers, SCP fallback state, text editor
  reads/writes, uploads, downloads, recursive transfers, delete inspection, and
  delete requests carry `contextID`.
- The status bar transfer queue current scope now clears finished transfers
  only for the active SFTP context.
- Command panel now supports `全部命令 / 当前服务器`.
- Default Command panel scope is `全部命令`; the last scope is remembered in
  local non-sensitive state.
- `全部命令` history queries all servers and shows source server badges.
- `当前服务器` history queries only the active server.
- `全部命令` favorites show all global/group/server favorites with source
  badges.
- `当前服务器` favorites show global plus the current server/group favorites.
- Command insert/execute still targets only the current active terminal and
  does not auto-switch or auto-connect another server.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify Terminal backend PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- SFTP remains an independent SSH transport/session and does not reuse
  Terminal or Monitor sessions.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.
- No passwords, private keys, passphrases, terminal contents, or remote file
  contents were added to logs or docs.

    sessions.

  - Server-wide SFTP stop still closes remaining contexts.
  - Command history `all` scope returns multiple servers and source names.
  - Command history `currentServer` scope only returns the selected server.
  - Command favorites `all` scope returns all scopes and joins server/group
    source names.
- Frontend:

    by terminal context.
  - TerminalWorkspace switches SFTP panel context when same-server terminal tabs
    switch.
  - Closing one terminal tab closes only its SFTP context and terminal session.
  - SFTP requests in panel/store tests now assert `contextID`.
  - Command panel default all-scope request, source badges, and current-server
    cache bucket are covered.

## 2026-06-17: SCP Fallback MVP

Baseline commit:
`2ab4c83d931468c33ec83f6b40e4de47aa77400f`

Stage goal:
Add SCP compatibility transfers for servers without an SFTP subsystem, such as
OpenWrt / ImmortalWrt / Dropbear environments. This is attached inside the
existing SFTP manager as a transfer backend mode and does not alter SSH
terminal, monitor, workspace state, `DisconnectServer`, SecretStore, Key Vault,
Backup / Import / Export, or Local Terminal quarantine.

### Completed

- Added SFTP/SCP mode metadata:
  - `SFTPState.mode`;
  - `SFTPState.capabilities`;
  - `SFTPTransferState.mode`;
  - `SFTPListResult.mode`.
- Added automatic SCP fallback when `OpenSFTP()` fails with unsupported
  subsystem style errors:
  - `SFTP_UNSUPPORTED`;
  - subsystem request failed;
  - remote closed during init;
  - missing `sftp-server` / `openssh-sftp-server`.
- SCP fallback keeps the independent SSH transport owned by the SFTP manager.
  It does not reuse terminal or monitor sessions.
- Added controlled SSH command sessions for SCP protocol execution with a
  context-aware cancellation path and no lingering command watcher goroutine.
- Implemented legacy SCP protocol support:
  - upload single file through `scp -t`;
  - download single file through `scp -f`;
  - upload directories through recursive `scp -r -t`;
  - download directories through recursive `scp -r -f`;
  - read/write SCP ack bytes;
  - read/write `C`, `D`, `E`, and `T` records.
- Added transfer progress for SCP:
  - byte progress;
  - current file;
  - file counts for recursive downloads as records are discovered;
  - cancel through existing `SftpCancelTransfer`.
- Added SCP conflict behavior:
  - local download conflicts reuse overwrite / skip / rename;
  - remote upload conflicts use shell `test -e` with quoted paths;
  - `ask` remains a pre-transfer decision path and returns a clear conflict.
- Added SCP path hardening:
  - rejects empty path, `/`, `.`, `..`, and parent traversal;
  - shell arguments are single-quoted;
  - remote SCP filenames containing separators, traversal, NUL, or newlines are
    rejected;
  - downloads are constrained to the selected local target directory.
- Frontend SFTP panel:
  - displays `SCP 兼容模式`;
  - does not render a fake file list;
  - keeps upload file/folder and download file/folder actions available;
  - disables listing-dependent actions: refresh listing, mkdir, rename, delete,
    remote editor, and properties/list browsing;
  - transfer status/popup shows `SCP` source.

### Limitations

- Shell-based remote listing is not implemented in this MVP.
- SCP mode is transfer-only with explicit remote path input.
- SCP mode does not support remote editor, delete, rename, mkdir, properties, or
  full browse.
- Pause/resume is not implemented.
- Symlink following is not implemented.
- OpenWrt/Dropbear real-host validation was not performed in this environment;
  the fallback trigger and SCP protocol are covered by unit tests.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH terminal runtime.
- Did not modify Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.
- SCP failure does not call `DisconnectServer`.
- SCP failure does not close SSH terminal or monitor sessions.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 37 files and 232 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

- SHA-256:
  `9E2014DADACBC6BFAAC02EDFFB6EA7034CBB02032E5A9D6328A9950519D223AB`

## 2026-06-17: SFTP Folder Icons and Recursive Delete

Baseline commit:
`31d1097da912972b6586f3a6a6e210f727f1289a`

Stage goal:
Improve SFTP file-list recognizability and add recursive directory deletion
inside the existing independent bottom SFTP runtime. This round does not touch
SSH terminal, monitor, workspace state, `DisconnectServer`, SecretStore, Key
Vault, Backup / Import / Export, or Local Terminal quarantine.

### Completed

- Added SFTP file-list visual identity:
  - parent row icon;
  - folder icon;
  - file icon;
  - symlink icon;
  - directory rows now display type text as `文件夹`.
- Replaced misleading directory context-menu wording:
  - removed `删除空目录`;
  - directory menu now shows `删除目录及内容...`;
  - file menu now shows `删除文件`.
- Added typed delete preview API:
  - `SftpInspectDelete`;
  - `SFTPInspectDeleteRequest`;
  - `SFTPInspectDeleteResponse`.
- Extended typed delete request with:
  - `paths`;
  - `recursive`;
  - compatibility with legacy single `path` / `isDir`.
- Added recursive directory deletion:
  - scans directories through the existing SFTP client;
  - deletes files and symlinks first;

- Multi-select delete now supports files plus directories in one confirmed
  recursive delete request.

  do not pull the UI back after navigation.
- Delete failures return visible Chinese errors and do not close the SFTP
  session or affect SSH terminal sessions.

## 2026-06-17: SFTP Recursive Transfer MVP

Baseline commit:
`48c47a8e9faa5b6044a93110391b918e1999dec0`

Stage goal:
Add recursive SFTP directory upload/download while keeping SFTP in its existing
independent runtime and without touching SSH terminal, monitor, workspace,
SecretStore, Key Vault, Backup / Import / Export, or Local Terminal quarantine.

### Completed

- Added typed recursive transfer API:
  - `SftpUploadDirectory`;
  - `SftpDownloadDirectory`;
  - `SelectLocalUploadDirectory`.
- Extended SFTP transfer state with:
  - `recursive`;
  - `sourceType`;
  - `currentFile`;
  - `filesTotal`;
  - `filesDone`;
  - `failedCount`;
  - `skippedCount`;
  - `cancelable`.
- Added recursive upload planning:
  - scans the local directory tree;
  - preserves relative directory structure;
  - converts Windows local separators to POSIX remote paths;
  - skips symlinks by default;
  - rejects `..` traversal.
- Added recursive download planning:
  - scans the remote directory tree through the existing SFTP client;
  - preserves relative directory structure under the selected local directory;
  - skips remote symlinks by default;
  - rejects remote traversal and local target escape.
- Added recursive transfer execution:
  - creates remote/local directories;
  - streams files one by one with the existing transfer buffer pattern;
  - updates current file, file count, byte progress, speed, skipped count, and
    failed count;
  - supports cancel through the transfer context only;
  - partial file failures mark the transfer as `partial_failed` without closing
    the SFTP session.
- `Stop(connectionID)` now cancels queued, planning, and running transfers for
  that server before closing that SFTP runtime.
- Frontend SFTP panel:
  - adds `上传文件夹` in the empty-area context menu and `更多` menu;
  - adds `下载目录` for directory context menus;
  - routes mixed file + directory selection downloads through file download and
    recursive directory download as appropriate;
  - resolves recursive `ask` conflict policy once per job, not once per file.
- Transfer status UI:
  - status bar and transfer popover show directory transfer markers;
  - show current file, file count, byte progress, failed/skipped counts;
  - cancel button works for queued/planning/running recursive transfers.
- Upload completion refresh remains path-aware and does not pull the user back
  after navigating away.
- Terminal status cleanup treats `partial_failed` as a terminal transfer state.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime.
- Did not modify Terminal runtime.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)` semantics.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Local Terminal remains default-disabled/quarantined.
- SFTP remains the independent SFTP runtime/session and does not reuse terminal
  or monitor sessions.
- Recursive cancel does not call `DisconnectServer`.
- Symlinks are skipped by default; symlink following is not implemented.
- SCP fallback is not implemented.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`: passed, 37 files and 223 tests.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`: passed with the existing Vite chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

## 2026-06-17: Quarantine Unstable Local Terminal Runtime

Baseline commit:
`71225aaeb90dc9713b11119f28bd5a78e20a98cd`

Stage goal:
Emergency quarantine for the Local Terminal module after real-machine retest
still showed app exit/crash on local tab close and shell `exit`, plus blank
terminal surface with no input.

### Final Handling Strategy

- Local Terminal is now disabled by default.
- It is not shown in the top `+` server menu.
- Its shell preference is hidden from Settings.
- Backend `OpenLocalTerminal` is feature-gated and returns:
  `LOCAL_TERMINAL_DISABLED: 本地终端暂未启用`.
- Disabled `OpenLocalTerminal` does not create a session, PTY, process, or
  shell.
- `CloseLocalTerminal` is safe in disabled mode and returns without touching
  app shutdown.
- Frontend late `localterminal:*` events are ignored while disabled.
- The only temporary enable path is:
  `SERVERPILOT_LOCAL_TERMINAL_EXPERIMENTAL=1`

### Root Cause / Diagnosis

- The previous code-level close-path fix did not close the user-machine issue.
  That means the current Windows local-terminal implementation cannot be
  treated as release-stable.
- Hard audit found no direct app-level path from `CloseLocalTerminal` or shell
  exit into `DisconnectServer`, `CloseAll`, `runtime.Quit`, `WindowClose`, or
  `os.Exit`.
- The Windows adapter does not call `GenerateConsoleCtrlEvent`,
  `CTRL_C_EVENT`, or `CTRL_BREAK_EVENT`; it uses `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`,
  `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`, and `HideWindow`.
- The blank/no-input failure was not proven fixed. The likely fault remains in
  the current embedded PTY path under real Wails GUI conditions, not in SSH,
  SFTP, Monitor, or server workspace code.
- Because real Windows GUI stability for Local Terminal was not proven, this
  round chooses quarantine over pretending the feature is fixed.

### Actually Completed

- Added backend feature gate in `internal/localterminal`:
  - `ExperimentalEnv`;
  - `ExperimentalEnabled`;
  - `ErrDisabled`;
  - `Capabilities.Enabled`.
- Added App API safeguards:
  - `OpenLocalTerminal` returns `LOCAL_TERMINAL_DISABLED` before touching the
    manager;
  - `WriteLocalTerminal` and `ResizeLocalTerminal` return disabled while gated;
  - `CloseLocalTerminal` is a safe no-op while gated;
  - `ListLocalTerminals` returns an empty list while gated.
- Added frontend feature gate:
  - app startup loads `GetLocalTerminalCapabilities`;
  - local terminal store defaults to disabled;
  - `+` menu hides Local Terminal unless backend says enabled+supported;
  - Settings hides Local Terminal shell preference unless enabled+supported;
  - disabled store ignores late state/output events;
  - disabled stale local tabs can be removed without backend calls.
- Added tests for disabled and experimental paths:
  - backend disabled open returns `ErrDisabled`;
  - disabled open does not require a runtime manager;
  - disabled close/list are safe;
  - capabilities default disabled and enable only with env var;
  - frontend default `+` menu hides Local Terminal;
  - Settings hides local terminal shell preferences while disabled;
  - experimental mode still shows the `+` menu entry and settings;
  - store blocks disabled opens and ignores late events;
  - store uses 80x24 fallback if rows/cols are 0;
  - Windows adapter source test rejects console control event usage.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor or SFTP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, CredentialResolver, or Backup /
  Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains independent and not serverID-based, but is quarantined
  by default.

### Key Files Changed

- `app.go`
- `app_test.go`
- `internal/domain/localterminal.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager_test.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_windows_test.go`
- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/stores/localTerminal.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/ServerPicker.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.vue`
- `frontend/src/types.ts`
- `frontend/src/api/backend.ts`
- `frontend/wailsjs/go/main/App.d.ts`
- `frontend/wailsjs/go/main/App.js`
- `frontend/wailsjs/go/models.ts`
- related frontend tests

## 2026-06-17: Local Terminal Close Lifecycle And Topbar Entry Fix

Baseline commit:
`60ba9eb75a90b2e4225e1abe59cbd59eee72a101`

Stage goal:
Fix the remaining real-machine regression where closing a Local Terminal tab or
letting the local shell exit could make ServerPilot close/crash, and remove the
permanent topbar Local Terminal shortcut. This was a regression fix only.

### Root Causes Found

- Code audit found no direct `CloseLocalTerminal -> DisconnectServer`,
  `CloseLocalTerminal -> CloseAll`, `CloseLocalTerminal -> runtime.Quit`, or
  `CloseLocalTerminal -> app shutdown` backend path.
- The backend Local Terminal event boundary was still unsafe: if the Wails event
  emitter panicked while sending `localterminal:output`, `localterminal:state`,
  or `localterminal:error`, the panic could escape the Local Terminal lifecycle.
  Shell exit is exactly when a final state event is emitted, so this was a
  realistic crash path.
- The Local Terminal tab close affordance used `.stop` but did not use
  `.prevent`. In a topbar made of tab buttons with nested close controls, this
  left default/click behavior less isolated than it needed to be.
- Closing the active Local Terminal tab could leave both local and SSH workspace
  activity empty even when adjacent tabs existed. That did not intentionally
  close the app, but it made the close path fragile and visually ambiguous.
- The permanent `WorkspaceTabs` Local Terminal button duplicated the `+` menu
  entry and kept a second open path alive.

### Actually Completed

- Wrapped Local Terminal output/state/error event dispatch in a safe emitter
  boundary. Event emitter panics are now recovered and logged as sanitized local
  terminal event-send failures instead of escaping a goroutine.
- Added backend lifecycle tests proving:
  - closing one Local Terminal does not cancel the manager root context;
  - shell exit does not cancel the manager root context;
  - closing one Local Terminal does not affect another Local Terminal;
  - event-emitter panic does not escape the Local Terminal lifecycle and the
    manager remains usable.
- Removed the permanent topbar Local Terminal shortcut from `WorkspaceTabs`.
  The only open entry is now the `+` server menu.
- Removed the now-unused `openLocalTerminal` event wiring from `WorkspaceTabs`
  instances in `App.vue`.
- Tightened Local Terminal tab close events with
  `pointerdown.stop.prevent`, `mousedown.stop.prevent`, and
  `click.stop.prevent`.
- Added active-tab fallback after closing an active Local Terminal: if adjacent
  tabs remain, ServerPilot activates the neighboring SSH workspace/local tab;
  if no tab remains, it stays in an empty workspace state instead of closing.
- Added frontend tests proving:
  - the `+` menu still has the Local Terminal entry;
  - the topbar permanent Local Terminal shortcut is gone;
  - closing a Local Terminal tab calls `CloseLocalTerminal`;
  - closing a Local Terminal tab does not emit `disconnectServer`;
  - close click/pointer events are prevented and do not bubble into tab
    activation;
  - closing an active local tab falls back to an adjacent server workspace.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor or SFTP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore, Key Vault, CredentialResolver, or Backup /
  Import / Export behavior.
- Local Terminal remains an independent runtime and is still not serverID-based.
- `CloseAll` remains app-shutdown-only behavior.

### Key Files Changed

- `internal/localterminal/manager.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager_test.go`
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/ServerPicker.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/style.css`

- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 37 files and

  chunk-size warning remains.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

## 2026-06-17: SSH Unicode And Local Terminal Process Isolation Fixes

Baseline commit:
`924f485be9fe4984c8ef069f3febe8afbf67eb05`

Stage goal:
Fix the four regressions still reproduced on the user's real machine:
Debian Oh My Zsh Chinese input showing `<ffffffff>`, Local Terminal opening an
external cmd/PowerShell window and destabilizing ServerPilot, missing compact
monitor restore splitter in collapsed mode, and the SSH terminal bottom black
strip above the SFTP splitter.

### Root Causes

- Chinese `<ffffffff>` was not proven end to end in the previous round. The
  frontend `TextEncoder` path was correct, but SSH shell startup did not attempt
  a UTF-8 locale. Oh My Zsh/ZLE can render valid UTF-8 bytes as invalid
  `<ffffffff>` cells when the remote PTY locale is non-UTF-8.
- The Local Terminal factory used the default `go-pty` command start path
  without explicit hidden-window and process-group isolation flags. On Windows
  this could expose a real console window and let child console lifecycle
  signals destabilize the GUI process.
- The compact monitor collapsed layout removed the splitter row entirely and
  moved restore into a text button, so the visible restore handle/arrow
  disappeared.
- Terminal bottom leftover pixels were still able to inherit non-terminal xterm
  helper or container backgrounds, leaving a dark strip above the SFTP splitter.

### Actually Completed

- Added SSH terminal UTF-8 locale setup before shell startup using SSH
  `Setenv`, not shell `export`:
  - `LC_CTYPE=C.UTF-8`
  - `LANG=C.UTF-8`
  - fallback to `en_US.UTF-8`
  - failures are ignored so terminal startup is not blocked.
- Added byte-level tests for the full SSH input chain:
  - API wrapper sends `dataBase64`;
  - `App.WriteTerminal` receives the Wails request;
  - terminal manager base64-decodes to bytes;
  - mock shell receives `啊` as `E5 95 8A`.
- Added SSH client tests proving locale setup calls `Setenv` and does not write
  shell-visible commands.
- Added hidden Windows Local Terminal process configuration for ConPTY:
  `CREATE_NO_WINDOW`, `CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP`, and `HideWindow`.
- Added Local Terminal read/write/wait panic guards. PTY/process panics now emit
  sanitized local terminal errors/states instead of escaping the goroutine.
- Restored compact monitor collapsed restore splitters:
  - `monitorCollapsed`: restore splitter row + mount pane;
  - `mountsCollapsed`: monitor pane + restore splitter row.
- Tightened terminal surface CSS so bottom leftover pixels and xterm helper
  elements use `--terminal-bg`, without adding padding or changing FitAddon
  sizing.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify Monitor or SFTP runtime.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export secret behavior.
- Did not implement SCP fallback, recursive transfer, Local Terminal History,
  Docker, port forwarding, macOS Keychain, or a new workspace model.

- `internal/sshclient/terminal.go`
- `internal/sshclient/terminal_test.go`

- `internal/localterminal/manager_test.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_windows.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_other.go`
- `internal/localterminal/process_windows_test.go`
- `frontend/src/api/backend.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/CompactMonitorSidebar.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/CompactMonitorSidebar.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/style.css`
- `frontend/src/style.tokens.test.ts`

## 2026-06-17: Terminal Unicode, Local Tabs, And Collapsed Layouts

Baseline commit:
`a65e979354e372755eae0057e4b722e7f62712af`

Stage goal:
Fix real-machine regressions found after the previous terminal/layout round.
This is not a feature stage and does not change the frozen runtime
architecture.

### Actually Completed

- SSH terminal input now uses one shared UTF-8 terminal encoder:
  `TextEncoder` -> UTF-8 bytes -> base64 -> `WriteTerminal`.
- Added byte-level regression coverage for Chinese input. `xterm.onData("啊")`
  must send bytes `E5 95 8A`, not `0xff`/Latin-1-truncated data.
- SSH and local terminal key handlers now track IME composition state and let
  composing events pass through before shortcut handling.
- Local terminal opening now has:
  - an App-level in-flight guard;
  - a store-level pending promise guard;
  - sessionID upsert for backend `localterminal:state` events.

  not call `DisconnectServer` or any app/window close path.

  - cmd preference uses `cmd.exe`.

  summary. The splitter sits directly above the right-side status bar.

  share the continuous `--terminal-bg` surface to remove the heavy black gap.

## 2026-06-18: Direct ConPTY Flag Conflict Decision

### Goal

Resolve the Windows Direct ConPTY `CREATE_NO_WINDOW` conflict and decide
whether Local Terminal can be exposed to users.

### Result

Local Terminal remains default-disabled.

The product decision is now behavior-based rather than flag-literal:
`CREATE_NO_WINDOW` is not required if real GUI smoke proves there is no external
PowerShell/cmd window and the embedded terminal has correct input/output.
However, this round did not complete a reliable enabled GUI smoke. The user
also clarified that the 4-second local terminal open/close log was likely from
manual operation, so it is not counted as Codex GUI smoke evidence.

### Current Direct ConPTY Flags

## 2026-06-18: Restore Windows Local Terminal Entry

### Goal

Restore the Windows Local Terminal entry after user true-machine validation and
remove the over-conservative automatic screenshot smoke gate.

### Result

Windows Local Terminal entry is restored. The app no longer hides Local
Terminal only because Codex automated screenshot/prompt recognition is
unstable.

### User-Confirmed Evidence

The user manually opened the Windows GUI build and confirmed:

- cmd can open.
- PowerShell can open.

This user GUI result is now treated as the product decision input. Automatic
visual smoke instability is documented as risk, not as a reason to keep the
entry hidden.

### Root Cause

The previous round left `ExperimentalEnabled()` tied to a closed release gate
because Codex could not reliably prove enabled GUI behavior by screenshots.
That made capabilities return disabled even though Direct ConPTY existed and
the user had manually confirmed cmd/PowerShell could open.

### Fix

- Replaced the smoke/release gate with runtime capability detection.
- Windows reports Local Terminal supported when:
  - Direct ConPTY APIs are available;
  - the platform is Windows;
  - shell resolver can find cmd or PowerShell.
- `+` menu shows `本地终端` when capabilities are supported.
- Settings shows the Local Terminal shell preference when supported.
- Settings shows a disabled Local Terminal card for unsupported platforms
  instead of silently hiding the section.
- Open failure protection remains: backend errors are surfaced, and frontend
  store does not create a blank local tab.
- Close and shell-exit lifecycle remains isolated from server workspaces.

### Architecture / Safety Notes

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Local Terminal still does not use `serverID`.
- Local Terminal still does not participate in `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- No terminal input/output is logged.

- `app.go`
- `app_test.go`
- `internal/localterminal/manager.go`

- `internal/localterminal/process_windows.go`
