## 2026-06-18: Local Terminal Productization

Baseline commit:
`b07a3f280abd6f099f8525a59b9bc1f2e008ad24`

Stage:
Productize the Windows Local Terminal entry after user true-machine validation.

Windows executable:
`build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`

Windows executable SHA-256:
`97AC02822C0BC0ED1E554F0DB4ADAFB91218559A0ADC6AD62A5BDD43175E404C`

### User-Confirmed Starting Point

The user had already confirmed on the real Windows GUI build:

- `+` menu had Local Terminal.
- Settings had the Local Terminal default shell selector.
- cmd opened, rendered output, and accepted input.
- PowerShell opened, rendered output, and accepted input.
- `echo serverpilot-local-terminal-smoke` worked.
- `exit` did not close ServerPilot.
- Closing a local terminal tab did not close ServerPilot.
- SSH / SFTP / SCP / Monitor remained normal.

### Root Cause / UX Problem

- Local Terminal was exposed as one generic entry plus a Settings-level default
  shell selector. That forced users to change settings just to choose cmd vs
  PowerShell.
- Local terminal tabs were rendered after all SSH/workspace tabs, so CMD and
  PowerShell looked pinned to the far right instead of participating in the
  normal tab sequence.
- Local Terminal had separate clipboard behavior from SSH terminal, creating
  inconsistent copy-on-select and right-click paste behavior.
- Shell process exit emitted local terminal state, but the tab remained visible
  until manually closed.

### Completed

- Removed the visible "本地终端默认 Shell" setting from Settings.
- Kept `localTerminalShellPreference` only as a compatibility field in the
  settings model.
- Replaced the single Local Terminal menu entry with direct `+` menu entries:
  - `CMD`
  - `PowerShell`
  - `CMD（管理员）`
  - `PowerShell（管理员）`
- Added typed Local Terminal shell fields:
  - `shellKind: cmd | powershell`
  - `elevated: boolean`
  - response/state `shellKind`, `shellName`, `elevated`, `title`, `status`
- `OpenLocalTerminal` now uses the menu-provided `shellKind`; it no longer
  reads Settings to decide the shell.
- Capabilities now expose Direct ConPTY availability, current process elevation,
  normal shell options, and admin shell options.
- CMD/PowerShell local tabs now participate in a unified `WorkspaceTabs`
  `tabOrder` with SSH terminal and workspace tabs.
- New local tabs append in normal creation order, and later SSH tabs can appear
  to their right.
- LocalTerminalView now reuses the same clipboard composable as SSH
  TerminalView.
- Local terminal copy-on-select follows the existing terminal setting.
- Local terminal right-click paste follows the existing terminal setting and
  writes through `WriteLocalTerminal`.
- Shell `exit` now auto-removes only the matching local terminal tab and
  selects an adjacent tab when needed.
- Late local terminal output/state cannot resurrect a closed/auto-removed tab.
- Admin local terminal strategy:
  - if ServerPilot itself is elevated, admin CMD/PowerShell opens through the
    same Direct ConPTY adapter;
  - if ServerPilot is not elevated, the app shows a Chinese explanation dialog,
    creates no fake tab, and does not spawn external cmd/PowerShell.

### Architecture / Safety

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Did not modify Command History / Favorites / Completion data model.
- Local Terminal still does not use `serverID`.
- Local Terminal still does not participate in `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Local Terminal still does not enter Backup / Export.
- Terminal input/output and clipboard content are not logged.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Backend tests cover:
  - menu-driven shell kind resolution for cmd and PowerShell;
  - capabilities not depending on screenshot smoke flags;
  - admin/elevation capability fields;
  - app open failure not leaving fake local sessions;
  - independent local session close.
- Frontend tests cover:
  - Settings no longer rendering Local Terminal default Shell;
  - `+` menu showing CMD / PowerShell / admin entries;
  - correct `OpenLocalTerminal(shellKind, elevated)` payload;
  - non-elevated admin entry using app explanation instead of backend open;
  - unified tab order: SSH, CMD, then later SSH remains in that order;
  - shell `exited` state removes only the matching local tab;
  - local copy-on-select;
  - local right-click paste through `WriteLocalTerminal`;
  - local tab close not calling `DisconnectServer`.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 39 files and
  279 tests. The existing jsdom canvas warning remains non-fatal.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.
- `git diff --check`: passed.

1. Open Settings and confirm "本地终端默认 Shell" is gone.
2. Confirm other terminal settings, including copy-on-select and right-click
   paste, remain visible.
3. Open the `+` menu and confirm it shows 添加服务器, 添加分组, CMD,
   PowerShell, CMD（管理员）, PowerShell（管理员）.
4. Click CMD and confirm a CMD tab opens.
5. Click PowerShell and confirm a PowerShell tab opens.
6. Confirm CMD/PowerShell tabs appear in the same tab list as server tabs.
7. Open a new server tab after CMD/PowerShell and confirm it appears to their
   right instead of forcing CMD/PowerShell to the far right.
8. Enable terminal copy-on-select and test selection copy in CMD.
9. Test selection copy in PowerShell.
10. Enable terminal right-click paste and test paste in CMD.
11. Test right-click paste in PowerShell.

13. Type `exit` in PowerShell and confirm only that tab closes automatically.

    open via embedded Direct ConPTY.

- macOS/Linux Local Terminal remains unsupported by this Windows ConPTY path.

## 2026-06-18 - Terminal clipboard interaction and SFTP toolbar layout polish

### Goal

- Fix terminal content selection/right-click paste behavior.
- Close the SSH terminal content custom context menu.
- Keep terminal tab context menus and SFTP file-list context menus unchanged.
- Merge the SFTP remote path input and toolbar actions into one compact row.

### Root Cause

- SSH TerminalView still used a terminal-content `ContextMenu`; right-click
  paste was skipped whenever xterm still had a selection.
- LocalTerminalView had the same selection guard, so CMD/PowerShell required
  clearing selection before right-click paste.
- SFTP kept the remote path input in a separate full-width row while the refresh
  action stayed at the far right of the toolbar.

### Completed

- SSH terminal content `contextmenu` now always prevents browser/default menu
  and stops propagation.
- SSH terminal content right-click no longer opens the custom content menu.
- When right-click paste is enabled, SSH terminal content right-click pastes
  through `terminal.paste()`, preserving the existing xterm `onData` input path.
- When right-click paste is disabled, SSH terminal content right-click does
  nothing and still shows no menu.
- LocalTerminalView uses the same behavior for CMD/PowerShell:
  - selection remains allowed;
  - right-click paste works even while selected text remains highlighted;
  - disabled right-click paste reads no clipboard and writes no local terminal
    input.
- `useTerminalClipboard` keeps copy-on-select shared and suppresses rapid
  duplicate writes of the same selection without logging clipboard content.
- SFTP toolbar now starts with:
  - remote path input;
  - refresh;
  - Up;
  - Home;
  - upload/download and remaining actions.
- Removed the duplicate standalone long SFTP path row.
- SFTP path input is constrained to a compact toolbar width with min/max bounds.
- Hidden-file active highlight remains unchanged.
- SCP compatibility mode uses the same toolbar/path layout.

### Architecture / Safety

- Did not modify SSH runtime architecture.
- Did not modify SSH Terminal PTY lifecycle.
- Did not modify Monitor runtime.
- Did not modify SFTP/SCP runtime architecture.
- Did not modify `DisconnectServer(serverID)`.
- Did not modify workspace state model.
- Did not modify SecretStore or Key Vault.
- Did not modify Backup / Import / Export.
- Did not modify Command History / Favorites / Completion data model.
- Did not modify Local Terminal runtime architecture.
- Clipboard content and terminal input/output are not logged.

### Tests Added / Updated

- SSH TerminalView tests now cover:
  - copy-on-select still writes selected text;
  - right-click paste works with no selection;
  - right-click paste also works while a selection remains active;
  - disabled right-click paste shows no content menu and performs no paste.
- LocalTerminalView tests now cover:
  - copy-on-select;
  - right-click paste through `WriteLocalTerminal`;
  - right-click paste while selection remains active;
  - disabled right-click paste reading no clipboard and writing no PTY input.
- SftpPanel tests now cover:
  - path input inside the toolbar;
  - refresh before Up;
  - Up before Home;
  - compact toolbar path class;
  - root disables Up by finding the actual Up button;
  - unbound/failed toolbar status text remains absent with the new order.

## 2026-06-18 - Terminal Paste Selection Fix + SSH Tunnel MVP

### Goal

- Fix terminal right-click paste leaving pasted content selected.
- Add SSH port forwarding / tunnel management MVP without changing SSH
  terminal, monitor, SFTP/SCP, Local Terminal, SecretStore, Key Vault, backup
  secret handling, or workspace architecture.

### Root Cause

- The shared terminal clipboard path pasted via xterm/local terminal input but
  did not explicitly clear xterm selection after paste.
- Copy-on-select could run close to a paste operation, so clearing selection
  needed a short suppression window to avoid re-copying stale selected text.
- Port forwarding did not yet have a dedicated runtime, Wails API, profile
  persistence, event store, or UI.

### Fix / Implementation

- `useTerminalClipboard` now:
  - suppresses copy-on-select briefly during paste;
  - clears selection and focuses before writing paste text;
  - clears selection and focuses again on the next frame.
- `TerminalView` and `LocalTerminalView` both use the shared paste behavior.
- Disabled right-click paste still prevents the terminal/browser context menu,
  clears selection, and refocuses the terminal without reading clipboard text.
- Added `internal/tunnelmanager`:
  - local forward listens locally and uses an independent SSH client to dial
    the remote target;
  - remote forward uses SSH remote listen and forwards incoming remote
    connections to a local target;
  - dynamic SOCKS5 listens locally and supports no-auth CONNECT only;
  - BIND/UDP are rejected;
  - stop closes listeners, active connections, and SSH transport;
  - events contain `serverID`, `tunnelID`, and timestamp.
- Added typed domain structs and Wails APIs:
  - `ListTunnelProfiles`, `CreateTunnelProfile`, `UpdateTunnelProfile`,
    `DeleteTunnelProfile`;
  - `ListTunnels`, `StartTunnel`, `StopTunnel`, `RestartTunnel`,
    `GetTunnelState`.
- Added SQLite migration version 9 for `tunnel_profiles`.
- Added tunnel profile metadata to backup/export/import. Runtime sessions,
  traffic counters, active connections, traffic payloads, and secrets are not
  included.
- Added top navigation and status-bar `端口转发` entry.
- Added `TunnelManagerDialog` for profile list, create/edit, start/stop/delete,
  local/remote/dynamic forms, traffic counters, and 0.0.0.0 confirmation.
- Extended `serverlifecycle.Coordinator` so `DisconnectServer(serverID)` stops
  that server's tunnel workers through the existing single lifecycle entry.

### Architecture Boundaries Kept

- Tunnel runtime uses a separate SSH client and does not reuse Terminal,
  Monitor, SFTP/SCP, or Local Terminal sessions.
- No tunnel code calls `DisconnectServer`; only the coordinator stops tunnels
  during server-wide disconnect.
- StopTunnel only stops the target tunnel.
- UI only calls typed APIs and consumes typed events.
- No tunnel traffic content, clipboard content, terminal input/output,
  passwords, passphrases, private keys, or SecretStore values are logged.
- SecretStore / Key Vault / Backup secret behavior was not changed.

### Tests Added / Updated

- Backend tunnel manager tests cover:
  - local tunnel start and independent dialer use;
  - local data forwarding;
  - local listener close on stop;
  - dynamic SOCKS5 CONNECT;
  - dynamic SOCKS5 BIND/UDP rejection;
  - remote forward start and remote listen rejection;
  - 0.0.0.0 confirmation requirement;
  - StopServer isolation by server ID.
- Persistence tests cover tunnel profile CRUD and backup payload profile
  metadata.
- Lifecycle tests cover `DisconnectServer(serverID)` stopping target server
  tunnels while leaving another server's tunnels untouched.
- Frontend tests cover:
  - SSH terminal paste clearSelection/focus behavior;
  - Local Terminal paste clearSelection/focus behavior;
  - top navigation `端口转发` entry;
  - tunnel store profile/runtime/event behavior;
  - tunnel dialog save/start and 0.0.0.0 confirmation.

### Validation

- `gofmt`: passed.
- `go vet ./...`: passed.
- `go test ./...`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check` from `frontend`: passed.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run` from `frontend`: passed, 41 files and
  289 tests. The existing jsdom canvas warning remains non-fatal.
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build` from `frontend`: passed with the existing Vite
  chunk-size warning.
- `wails build`: passed for Windows amd64.

- Path: `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256:
  `E5B5C7A72803C8182708ED9B708438BD25C3E96D5AF6A438AA82230E9A1E54B4`

### Manual Verification Checklist

1. SSH terminal: select text, right-click paste, confirm pasted content is not
   selected afterward.
2. SSH terminal: confirm cursor/focus returns to the end of pasted content.
3. CMD local terminal: repeat right-click paste with and without active
   selection.
4. PowerShell local terminal: repeat right-click paste with and without active
   selection.

### Risk / Unfinished

  unsupported in this MVP.

  `GatewayPorts`.
- Auto-start tunnel profiles were not implemented.

## 2026-06-18 - Docker Container Management MVP

### Current Status

- Implemented Docker / container management MVP through SSH exec.
- Built Windows EXE:
  `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256:
  `486053972EB7C3381898D1229DC64FD7291157E31087D7D096BD74E25BDDDA1A`

### Scope Completed

- Docker availability check:
  - runs `docker version --format '{{json .}}'`;
  - maps not installed / permission denied / daemon stopped errors to Chinese
    user messages.
- Container list:
  - runs `docker ps -a --format '{{json .}}'`;
  - parses JSON lines in Go;
  - front-end receives typed `DockerContainer` rows.
- Container operations:
  - start;
  - stop;
  - restart;
  - remove stopped containers only.
- Running/paused/restarting containers are blocked from delete with:
  `该容器正在运行，请先停止后再删除。`
- Logs:
  - recent log read with configurable tail line count;
  - real-time follow via `docker logs -f`;
  - UI memory cap at 5000 lines;
  - stop follow on dialog close/server switch.
- Stats:
  - one-shot `docker stats --no-stream --format '{{json .}}'`;
  - optional stats watcher;
  - stop watcher on dialog close/server switch.
- Inspect:
  - summary only;
  - displays ID, name, image, created, state, status, ports, mount count,
    network names, restart policy;
  - does not expose Env or full inspect output.
- UI:
  - top menu entry `容器管理`;
  - floating dialog, not a workspace switch;
  - server selector, search, running/stopped filter, refresh;
  - start/stop/restart/delete/logs/stats/info actions;
  - dangerous operation confirmations for stop/restart/delete.

### Architecture / Security Boundaries

- Added `internal/dockermanager` as an independent SSH exec subsystem.
- Docker manager does not reuse Terminal, SFTP/SCP, Monitor, Tunnel, or Local
  Terminal sessions.
- Docker operation failure does not affect SSH Terminal, SFTP/SCP, Monitor,
  Tunnel, or Local Terminal runtimes.
- `DisconnectServer(serverID)` now stops Docker log streams and stats watchers
  through the existing lifecycle coordinator resource hook.
- Stop log stream / stats watcher does not call `DisconnectServer`.
- Container ID is validated with a strict pattern before command construction.
- Container logs, full inspect output, Env, stats, and Docker runtime state are
  not written to SQLite, app logs, or backup/export.
- No SSH credential, password, passphrase, private key, SecretStore value,
  terminal IO, file content, or container log content is logged.
- Backup/import/export secret behavior was not changed.

## 2026-06-19 - Local Terminal Admin Preference And Docker List Layout Fix

### Goal

Fix two user-reported UI issues:

1. Remove separate `CMD（管理员）` / `PowerShell（管理员）` entries from the
   `+` menu and move admin-mode choice into Settings.
2. Stabilize Docker multi-container list layout so text, stats, status, and
   action buttons no longer overlap or drift after multi-select or stats
   refresh.

### Root Cause

- Local Terminal admin mode was exposed as separate menu entries. That made the
  `+` menu noisy and split shell selection from privilege preference.
- Settings did not persist a dedicated admin-mode preference, so `elevated`
  could only come from the menu click payload.
- Docker container cards used a compact two-row grid with status and stats
  sharing one column while actions used content-sized wrapping. In dense lists,
  long image/name/status text and stats refresh could squeeze neighboring
  blocks and make rows look overlapped.

### Fix

- Added non-sensitive setting `localTerminalElevatedEnabled`.
- Bumped settings model version to 6.
- Added SQLite migration 10:
  `local_terminal_elevated_enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`.
- Updated Settings UI with:
  `以管理员模式打开本地终端`.
- Removed `CMD（管理员）` and `PowerShell（管理员）` from `ServerPicker`.
- `ServerPicker` now emits only shell kind: `cmd` or `powershell`.
- `App.vue` now derives `elevated` from
  `settings.localTerminalElevatedEnabled`.
- If admin mode is enabled while ServerPilot is not elevated, UI asks for
  confirmation, calls the elevated relaunch API, and no fake/blank tab is
  created.
- Elevated relaunch uses Windows `ShellExecuteW runas`.
- The elevated process consumes a startup local-terminal request once and then
  opens the selected CMD/PowerShell tab.
- If ServerPilot is already elevated, opening CMD/PowerShell passes
  `elevated=true` to Local Terminal.
- Docker container cards now use a stable five-column grid:
  checkbox / main info / status / resources / actions.
- Added dedicated `.container-name`, `.container-image`, and `.container-id`
  classes with ellipsis behavior.
- Kept compact two-row responsive layout for narrow windows.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profiles & Preferences MVP

### Goal

Implement configurable terminal profiles and preferences without changing the
frozen runtime architecture.

### Scope

- Terminal profile data model and SQLite persistence.
- Global default terminal profile.
- Per-server terminal profile override.
- SSH and Local Terminal xterm appearance application.
- Settings UI for profile CRUD and preview.
- Server edit form profile selector.
- Standard backup/export/import support for non-sensitive terminal profiles.

### Implementation

- Added `TerminalProfile` domain model and validation.
- Added SQLite migration 11:
  - `terminal_profiles` table.
  - `app_settings.default_terminal_profile_id`.
  - `connections.terminal_profile_id`.
- Updated settings model to version 7.
- Added typed Wails APIs:
  - `ListTerminalProfiles`
  - `CreateTerminalProfile`
  - `UpdateTerminalProfile`
  - `DuplicateTerminalProfile`
  - `DeleteTerminalProfile`
  - `SetDefaultTerminalProfile`
  - `AssignServerTerminalProfile`
  - `GetResolvedTerminalProfile`
- Added frontend terminal profile store and xterm option mapper.
- Added Settings -> Terminal profile manager:
  - profile list;
  - new/copy/delete;
  - save profile;
  - set global default;
  - apply to open terminals;
  - live preview.
- Added server edit form selector:
  - inherit global default;
  - choose a terminal profile override.
- SSH Terminal resolves:
  - server override first;
  - global default otherwise.
- Local Terminal uses the global default profile.
- Applying a profile to open terminals updates xterm options and refits the
  view. It does not dispose xterm, reconnect SSH, recreate PTY, or restart
  Local Terminal processes.
- Standard backup/export/import now includes terminal profiles, default profile
  ID, and server profile mappings. Import remaps custom profile IDs and keeps
  built-in `default` mapped to the local default profile.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Delete Semantics And Settings Action Style

### Baseline

- Previous commit: `9f65805f9302922f31fa3feb865ce1cd772a1ad9`
  (`fix: improve terminal profile delete and settings header layering`).
- Task scope: Terminal Profile deletion semantics and Settings header button
  style only.
- Architecture freeze v1.0 remained in effect.

### User Feedback

1. The red `删除` button at the bottom of the Terminal Profile editor was
   redundant.
2. Non-global-default Profiles should be deletable even if servers currently
   reference them, after a warning and confirmation.
3. The Settings top-right `保存` and `保存并关闭` button colors should be
   swapped.

### Root Cause

- The previous round made Profile deletion obvious in the list, but left the
  old editor action-row delete button in place.
- Store-level `DeleteTerminalProfile` still treated server usage as a hard
  delete blocker.
- Settings header action styling still treated `保存` as the primary action
  instead of `保存并关闭`.

### Fix

- Removed the editor action-row `删除` button.
- Kept the row-level Profile list `删除` button as the only delete entry.
- Default Profile / active global default Profile remains disabled for
  deletion.
- Added typed backend request/response:
  - `DeleteTerminalProfileRequest`
  - `DeleteTerminalProfileResponse`
- Backend delete now supports `forceDetachServers`.
- If a non-default Profile is in use and `forceDetachServers=false`, backend
  returns `TERMINAL_PROFILE_IN_USE`.
- When user confirms deleting an in-use Profile, frontend calls delete with
  `forceDetachServers=true`.
- Store executes server-detach and Profile deletion in one transaction:
  - `connections.terminal_profile_id = NULL`
  - delete terminal profile
- Affected servers now inherit the global default Profile after deletion.
- Existing open terminals are not reset, rebuilt, or disconnected.
- App refreshes the server list after a delete detaches server Profile
  references.
- Settings top-right button emphasis is swapped:
  - `保存并关闭`: primary blue button
  - `保存`: secondary button
- Ctrl+S still triggers save only.
- Save-and-close still closes Settings only after save succeeds.

### Files Changed

- `app.go`
- `internal/domain/terminal_profile.go`
- `internal/persistence/terminal_profiles.go`
- `internal/persistence/store_test.go`

## 2026-06-23 Alert settings layout and default window height fix

结论：
- 修复设置 -> 告警页面错位问题，并将真实默认窗口高度从 1500 调整为 1950，正好增加 30%。
- 默认窗口宽度保持 1360；已保存窗口尺寸继续优先，不强制最大化或全屏。
- 本轮只改 UI 布局、窗口默认值、测试和开发规则；未修改告警状态机或 runtime。

改动：
- 告警规则区从旧的松散 grid 改为统一表格式布局，列为 `规则 / 启用 / 阈值 / 持续`。
- 离线、CPU、内存、根分区、延迟规则使用相同 DOM 结构；checkbox、阈值、持续时间严格按列对齐。
- 离线规则阈值列显示 `—`，持续时间仍在持续列，避免跑到 CPU 等其他规则行。
- 百分比阈值输入框固定 88px，延迟阈值固定 96px，持续时间固定 84px；单位 `% / ms / 秒` 放在输入框外。
- `服务器告警` 和 `恢复时通知` 改为紧凑同区块开关；`发送测试告警` 移到告警标题区右侧。
- 窄窗口下规则行切换为紧凑两行布局，仍保持输入框合理宽度并避免横向滚动。
- 新增表单控件尺寸规则到 `Codex开发规则.md`，并在 `AGENTS.md` 兼容入口同步说明。
- 将窗口默认尺寸常量收敛到 `domain.DefaultWindowWidth` / `domain.DefaultWindowHeight`，Wails options、设置默认值和窗口 fallback 共用。
- 更新初始设置迁移默认 `window_height` 为 1950；不覆盖已有用户保存的窗口尺寸。

验证：
- `gofmt -w main.go window_state.go window_state_test.go internal/domain/models.go internal/persistence/store.go`：通过。
- `go vet ./...`：通过。
- `go test ./...`：通过。
- `cmd.exe /c npm run type-check`（在 `frontend` 目录执行）：通过。
- `cmd.exe /c npm run test -- --run`（在 `frontend` 目录执行）：通过，56 个测试文件、582 个测试；既有 jsdom canvas `getContext` 提示非致命。
- `cmd.exe /c npm run build`（在 `frontend` 目录执行）：通过，保留既有 Vite chunk-size warning。
- `wails build`：Windows amd64 通过。
- `git diff --check`：通过。

EXE：
- `build/bin/ServerPilot.exe`
- SHA-256：`D60DCB704C223DC2C11BF1251978D179A7DAE0EE1E1D860CF3C2E5AD10F21018`

提交：
- 待提交：`fix: align alert settings and increase default window height`

用户手测：
1. 打开设置 -> 告警。
2. 确认 `服务器告警` 和 `恢复时通知` 对齐在紧凑开关区域。
3. 确认规则以表格式排列。
4. 确认 CPU、内存、根分区、延迟 checkbox 在同一列。
5. 确认所有阈值输入框在同一列。
6. 确认所有持续时间输入框在同一列。
7. 确认服务器离线的持续时间没有跑到其他规则行。
8. 确认阈值输入框明显缩短。
9. 输入 100%，确认显示完整。
10. 输入延迟 5000 ms，确认显示完整。
11. 输入持续 600 秒，确认显示完整。
12. 测试开启 / 关闭各规则。
13. 保存设置并重新打开，确认值保留。
14. 点击发送测试告警。
15. 确认告警中心和 Toast 正常。
16. 在首次启动或无保存窗口尺寸环境下确认默认窗口高度约增加 30%，宽度不变。
17. 确认默认窗口不是最大化或全屏。
18. 缩小窗口，确认告警页面仍可滚动且不重叠。
19. 确认 SSH/SFTP/Monitor/Docker/Process/ServiceManager 正常。

风险：
- 已保存窗口尺寸的用户不会被新默认高度覆盖；如需体验新默认高度，需要手动调整窗口或清除保存尺寸。
- 本轮未改告警状态机、Monitor、Network Details、HostKeyPolicy、SSH runtime、`DisconnectServer(serverID)`、SecretStore / Key Vault、Backup secret 行为。

## 2026-06-22 Network Details high-connection and upload counter fix

结论：
修复 Network Details 中上传列为空、大连接数被表格行数拖低、`th_ecmanager` outbound-only 连接重复拆行的问题。现在 UI 明确区分 `Socket连接` 和 `连接跟踪` 两种口径。

改动：
- 后端远端脚本先做 socket count-only / remote-IP 聚合，再限制 raw socket rows 为前 500 行，summary 不再从前端表格行数计算。
- 新增 conntrack summary，优先读取 `/proc/net/nf_conntrack`，再读取 `/proc/net/ip_conntrack`，最后尝试 `conntrack -L`；只返回 count，不返回原始 conntrack 内容。
- outbound-only 连接按 `processName + protocol` 聚合，同程序多 PID 显示 `多个`，连接数、远程 IP 数、下载和估算上传按聚合行累加。
- `bytes_sent` 显示真实上传；`bytes_sent` 缺失但存在 `bytes_acked` 时显示 `≈` 估算上传，并单独写入 `uploadedBytesEstimate`。
- `bytes_received` 保持服务器视角下载语义；不使用接口总流量、Send-Q、Recv-Q、segment/packet 计数伪造进程流量。
- Network Inspection 日志只增加 sanitized count 诊断，不记录真实 IP/端口/raw socket output/cmdline/environ/credential。
- 未修改 SSH terminal runtime、SFTP/SCP runtime、Monitor runtime、`DisconnectServer(serverID)`、SecretStore / Key Vault、Backup secret 行为。

用户手测：
1. 连接这台 CentOS 服务器。
2. 打开网络详情。
3. 开启或手动刷新端口与连接。
4. 确认顶部显示 `Socket连接`、`远程 IP`、`连接跟踪`，不再只有含糊的“当前连接”。
5. 对比爱快路由系统的 100000+ 连接数；如果 CentOS 提供 conntrack，确认 `连接跟踪` 数量更接近该口径。
6. 如果 CentOS 不提供 conntrack，确认 `连接跟踪` 显示 `—`，不会显示假 0。
7. 上传文件到服务器，确认服务器接收方向显示在“下载”列。
8. 如果远端提供 `bytes_sent`，确认“上传”列显示真实值；如果只提供 `bytes_acked`，确认显示 `≈` 估算值。
9. 确认不再出现大量 `th_ecmanager` 每行 1 个连接的重复行。
10. 确认 `th_ecmanager` 聚合行连接数变大，PID 可显示 `多个`。
11. 确认 UI 不因 100000+ 连接卡死。
12. 确认 SSH terminal 和 SFTP 上传不受影响。
13. 确认 OpenWrt logread 和 Docker Compose 没有新增。

风险：
- 爱快路由看到的 100000+ 通常是 conntrack/NAT 会话口径，不等于本机进程 socket 表格口径；本轮分开显示，避免混淆。
- conntrack 无法可靠关联 PID/程序，本轮不把 conntrack 混入 endpoint rows。
- 上传列只在远端提供 `bytes_sent` 或可标记估算的 `bytes_acked` 时显示；不会用接口总上传流量伪造单进程上传。

## 2026-06-22 Network Details / 监听端口与连接 MVP

结论：
完成 Network Details MVP。首页左侧网络曲线现在打开“网络详情”，默认进入“端口与连接”Tab；Network Diagnostics 已整合为内部 Tab，不再从 App 根模板打开独立旧诊断浮窗。

改动：
- 新增 `internal/networkinspect` attachment layer，使用独立 SSH exec/context 获取端口和连接快照。
- 新增 typed Wails API：`OpenNetworkInspectionContext`、`GetNetworkEndpointSnapshot`、`CloseNetworkInspectionContext`。
- 支持 `ss`、`netstat`、`/proc/net/*` fallback；`/proc` fallback 只读取 `/proc/[pid]/comm`，不读取 `cmdline` / `environ`。
- 端口表显示 PID、程序、协议、监听 IP、端口、唯一远程 IP 数、连接数、上传、下载。
- 上传/下载只在 `ss -i` 提供 `bytes_sent` / `bytes_received` 时显示；缺失显示 `—`，不伪造 0。
- Network Diagnostics 拆出 `NetworkDiagnosticsPanel.vue` 并嵌入 `NetworkDetailsDialog.vue`。
- 顶部菜单“网络诊断”改为“网络详情”；Dashboard 旧诊断快捷入口打开 Network Details 的诊断 Tab。
- 首页网络接口选择框收窄到 92px；网络卡片最小高度提高到 178px；曲线区域高度提高到 76px。
- DisconnectServer 生命周期只负责取消对应 server 的 Network Inspection context，不改变原有断开语义。

1. 连接一台 Linux 服务器。

3. 确认打开“网络详情”。
4. 确认默认进入“端口与连接”，不是网络诊断。
5. 确认看到 PID、程序、协议、监听 IP、端口。

8. 支持时确认上传/下载累计字节显示；不支持时确认显示 `—`，不显示假数据。

13. 切换到“网络诊断”。
14. 测试 Ping / Traceroute / DNS / TCP。
15. 确认关闭网络详情时诊断任务终止。
16. 查看首页左侧网络卡片，确认网卡选择框变窄、卡片和曲线区域增高。
17. 点击网卡下拉，确认不会误开网络详情。
18. 测试 OpenWrt/BusyBox netstat fallback。
19. 确认 SSH terminal、Monitor、SFTP、Tunnel、Docker、Process Manager、系统服务、Local Terminal 正常。
20. 确认 OpenWrt procd `logread` 没有新增。
21. 确认 Docker Compose 没有新增。
22. 确认 `build/上传服务器说明.rtf` 没有恢复。

风险：
- 非 root 用户可能无法看到部分 PID / 程序名 / 字节计数，UI 会显示 `权限受限` 或 `—`。

## 2026-06-20 - 终端 Ctrl+滚轮真实 WebView 后续修复

### 目标

修复用户在 `30ed32e` 构建后确认的严重回归：真实 Windows Wails WebView 中，SSH terminal 内 Ctrl+鼠标滚轮仍然没有任何可见效果。

### 根因

- 上一版在部分路径上仍依赖 `WheelEvent.ctrlKey` / `metaKey`。真实 WebView2 中，物理 Ctrl+滚轮可能出现 wheel 事件本身没有可靠 Ctrl 状态。
- 终端组件没有使用 xterm 自带的 `attachCustomWheelEventHandler`，如果应用级 guard 没有准确消费事件，xterm 仍会把 wheel 当普通滚动处理。
- 项目使用 `@xterm/xterm 6.0.0`；可靠的公开字号更新 API 是 `terminal.options.fontSize = value`，不是旧式 `setOption` 路径。

### 实现

- `App.vue`
  - 在 capture 阶段跟踪 Ctrl/Meta keydown 和 keyup。
  - 窗口 blur 时重置修饰键状态，避免 Ctrl/Meta 卡住。
- `webviewZoomGuard.ts`
  - Ctrl/Cmd wheel 判断现在同时接受 `event.ctrlKey`、`event.metaKey`、
    `getModifierState('Control')`、`getModifierState('Meta')` 和物理按键跟踪状态。
  - 继续阻止整个 WebView 页面缩放，并按 terminal session ID 派发终端缩放。
- `TerminalView.vue` / `LocalTerminalView.vue`
  - 共用 terminal wheel zoom consume 路径。
  - 注册 xterm 的 `attachCustomWheelEventHandler`。
  - 消费缩放手势后返回 `false`，避免 xterm 继续按普通滚动处理同一个 wheel。
- `terminalZoom.ts`
  - 直接写入 `terminal.options.fontSize`。
  - 如果 xterm 实例暴露 `refresh()` 和 `rows`，刷新可见行。

### 测试新增 / 更新

- WebView zoom guard 测试覆盖：
  - Ctrl 已通过 keydown 跟踪，但 wheel 事件 `ctrlKey: false`；
  - Ctrl keyup 和 blur reset helper 后不再误判为缩放；
  - `getModifierState('Control')` fallback。
- Terminal zoom 测试覆盖 xterm 6 直接 `options.fontSize` 修改和行刷新。
- SSH Terminal / Local Terminal 测试直接覆盖 xterm custom wheel interception。
- 原有 capture 阶段终端滚轮测试保留。

### 修改文件

- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/TerminalView.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/LocalTerminalView.vue`
- `frontend/src/utils/webviewZoomGuard.ts`
- `frontend/src/utils/terminalZoom.ts`
- `frontend/src/utils/webviewZoomGuard.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/utils/terminalZoom.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/TerminalView.resize.test.ts`

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile List Create Action Layout Polish

### Goal

Fix only the Settings -> Terminal Profile left-list layout:

1. Keep the default Profile at the top of the list.
2. Move `新建` to the bottom of the Profile list.
3. Make `新建` and row `删除` buttons visually consistent and aligned.

### Root Cause

- The previous UI placed `新建` in a Profile list header, which put it above the
  default Profile row.
- Row delete buttons and the create button used different padding/sizing, so
  they did not line up cleanly.
- The Profile list did not have a footer area for create actions.

### Fix

- Added a Settings-level computed Profile list order that moves the built-in
  `default` Profile to the first row without changing the store, backend, data
  model, or migrations.
- Replaced the Profile list header action with:
  - `terminal-profile-list-scroll` for Profile rows;
  - `terminal-profile-list-footer` for the `新建` action.
- `新建` now appears after all Profile rows and stays in the left Profile list
  area.
- Added shared `profile-action-button` sizing for create/delete actions:
  40 px height, 72 px minimum width, centered content, no wrapping.
- Kept delete buttons danger-styled and right-aligned.
- Kept disabled delete buttons at the same size to avoid row layout jumps.
- Preserved long Profile name ellipsis.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Create Item Ordering And Action Order Polish

### Goal

Correct the Terminal Profile list semantics and editor action order:

1. `新建` must be the last item in the Profile list, not a fixed footer.
2. `新建` must directly follow the last Profile row without a large blank gap.
3. Editor bottom actions must be ordered:
   `复制`, `设为全局默认`, `应用到已打开终端`, `保存 Profile`.

### Root Cause

- The prior fix used a separate scroll area plus footer. That made `新建` stay
  at the bottom of the list container instead of directly after the last
  Profile row.
- The Profile editor action row still put the primary save action first, while
  the requested flow wants save last.

### Fix

- Removed the `terminal-profile-list-scroll` / `terminal-profile-list-footer`
  split.
- Rendered Profile rows and the create item in the same `.terminal-profile-list`
  flow.
- `terminal-profile-create-item` is now the last list item after all Profile
  rows.
- The Profile list keeps `align-content: start`, so `新建` is not pushed to the
  physical bottom of the container.
- The list may scroll as one body; `新建` scrolls with the Profile rows and
  remains after the last Profile.
- Kept the built-in default Profile sorted first in the Settings UI only.
- Kept shared `profile-action-button` sizing for `新建` and `删除`.
- Reordered editor action buttons to:
  1. `复制`
  2. `设为全局默认`
  3. `应用到已打开终端`
  4. `保存 Profile`
- `保存 Profile` remains primary.

### Files Changed

- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/style.css`
- `frontend/src/style.tokens.test.ts`

- `DEV_PROGRESS.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

- Settings tests verify:
  - with only default Profile, order is `default`, `新建`;
  - with multiple custom Profiles, order is `default`, custom Profiles, `新建`;
  - `新建` is no longer in a footer;
  - saving a newly created Profile inserts that Profile above `新建`;
  - `新建` continues to move down after the newly saved Profile;
  - `新建` and row `删除` share the same action-button class;

  - copy / set-default / apply / save paths remain covered.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Delete And Settings Header Layering Fix

### Baseline

- Previous commit: `0b133975a8732cab2252e8571172097181f72c9e`
  (`fix: apply terminal profiles live and improve settings actions`).
- Task scope: Terminal Profile delete affordance and Settings overlay header
  layering only.
- Architecture freeze v1.0 remained in effect.

### User Feedback

1. Custom Terminal Profile delete entry was not obvious enough.
2. Settings top Save / Save and Close / Close action area was visible, but
   scrolling content could leak text behind or above it.

### Root Cause

- Profile deletion was mainly exposed in the editor action row, not in each
  profile list row, so the affordance was easy to miss.
- Settings overlay used one scrolling panel and relied on a sticky header with
  a semi-transparent background and negative margins. That allowed content to
  scroll behind the header layer.

### Fix

- Added a visible `删除` button to the right side of every Terminal Profile row.
- Default Profile and the active global default are disabled for deletion with
  explanatory titles.
- Profiles referenced by current server configs are disabled in the list when
  Settings receives the current connection list.
- Backend delete blocking remains the fallback, with the existing Chinese
  toast/error path.
- Row delete clicks use `stopPropagation`, so they do not accidentally select
  the profile row.
- Delete confirmation now uses the existing AppDialogHost flow with:
  `确定删除终端配置「Profile 名称」吗？`
- Delete success refreshes the profile list and falls back to the default
  profile.
- Settings overlay panel now uses explicit flex layers:
  - fixed header;
  - scrolling main/category area.
- Settings header now has a solid background, border, and shadow.

## 2026-06-19 - Docker Container List Layout Fix

### Goal

Fix two Docker manager UI layout regressions only:

1. Container rows were incomplete in both single-container and multi-container
   lists; right-side action buttons could be clipped.
2. The `当前服务器` selector in the Docker toolbar was too wide.

### Root Cause

- The Docker body allowed the left list column to shrink to `420px`, while each
  container card required far more width because fixed columns plus gaps and
  padding exceeded the available list width.
- `.container-actions` was grouped with clipped columns and used
  `overflow: hidden`, so action buttons could disappear when the row was
  squeezed.
- The toolbar used `label:first-child { flex: 1 1 320px; }`, causing the
  current-server selector to stretch across the row.

### Fix

- Added explicit toolbar classes:
  `.docker-server-select-field`, `.docker-search-field`,
  `.docker-filter-field`.
- Set the current-server selector to a compact responsive width:
  `flex: 0 1 420px`, `max-width: 520px`, `min-width: 260px`.
- Rebalanced `.docker-body` to:
  `minmax(640px, 1fr) minmax(340px, 420px)`.
- Added `min-width: 0` to body and panels to prevent grid overflow.
- Set the Docker list panel to `overflow-x: hidden` and vertical scrolling.
- Rebuilt container rows as a stable five-column grid:
  checkbox / main info / status / resources / actions.
- Reduced fixed card columns and moved long name/image/status/ports text into
  ellipsis-safe columns.
- Removed `.container-actions` from clipped overflow and changed it to a visible
  wrapping flex strip.
- Added responsive fallbacks:
  - body becomes one column at `1120px`;
  - cards use explicit two-row areas at `760px`;
  - very narrow cards stack actions at `620px`.

### Files Changed

- `frontend/src/components/DockerManagerDialog.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/DockerManagerDialog.test.ts`
- `AI_BRIEF.md`
- `DEV_PROGRESS.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

### Tests Added / Updated

- Frontend tests now cover:
  - compact current-server selector and toolbar alignment classes;
  - single-container and multi-container action areas;
  - running container stop/restart buttons remaining present;
  - stopped container start/remove buttons remaining present;
  - long container name and image title/ellipsis-safe structure;
  - CSS layout contracts for body grid, card columns, `overflow-x: hidden`,
    `.container-actions { overflow: visible; }`, and narrow media fallbacks.

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Apply And Settings Action UX Fix

### Goal

Fix user acceptance issues in Terminal Profiles and Settings:

1. "Apply to open terminals" did not visibly update already-open terminals.
2. Font configuration required manually typing a font family.
3. Settings Save/Close actions were inconvenient while scrolling.

### Root Cause

- Profile apply only bumped a frontend revision and depended on prop refresh;
  unsaved profile edits were not persisted first, and already-mounted xterm
  instances were not targeted directly.
- xterm options were updated indirectly through component props instead of a
  reliable open-terminal registry.
- Font family was a raw text input only.
- Settings used a separate sticky save strip instead of a fixed header action
  area with save/close controls.

### Fix

- Added a frontend terminal instance registry for SSH and Local Terminal.
- TerminalView and LocalTerminalView register on mount and unregister on
  unmount/tab close.
- Registry entries track session ID, kind, server ID, resolved profile ID, and
  whether the terminal inherits the global default profile.
- Applying a profile now calls each matching terminal's xterm option update
  callback directly.
- Apply first validates and saves unsaved profile edits, then applies the
  backend-returned latest profile.
- The apply toast now reports:
  - `已应用到 N 个已打开终端`, or
  - `没有需要应用的已打开终端`.
- xterm options updated in place:
  font family, font size, line height, letter spacing, cursor style, cursor
  blink, scrollback, and theme.
- Terminal host background CSS variable is synced with the profile background.
- Existing fit scheduling is reused so changed rows/cols trigger the existing
  PTY resize path.
- Font UI is now a preset dropdown with a Custom option.
- Added client and backend fontFamily validation for unsafe CSS characters.
- Settings header now contains sticky Save, Save and Close, unsaved-change
  indicator, and close X.
- Ctrl+S saves Settings.
- Esc triggers the existing AppDialogHost unsaved-change flow.
- Save and Close is handled by App-level save and only closes after save
  succeeds.

### Files Changed

- `frontend/src/utils/terminalInstanceRegistry.ts`
- `frontend/src/utils/terminalProfile.ts`
- `frontend/src/stores/terminalProfiles.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/TerminalView.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/LocalTerminalView.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.vue`

## 2026-06-19 - Terminal Profile Create Placement And Server Tab Edit

### Goal

Fix two UI / interaction regressions only:

1. Move the Terminal Profile `新建` action into the Profile list area.
2. Add `编辑` to connected SSH workspace / terminal tab context menus.

### Root Cause

- The Terminal Profile create button lived in the whole Profile section header,
  visually far away from the left Profile list it operates on.
- `WorkspaceTabs` had close / reconnect / disconnect menu actions, but no
  event for opening the existing server edit dialog from a connected tab.
- Saving an edited connected server refreshed persisted config, but open tab
  labels were not explicitly synced from the saved connection result.

### Fix

- Moved `terminal-profile-new` into a new `.terminal-profile-list-header` inside
  the left Profile list.
- Removed the isolated Profile `新建` button from the right side of the section
  header.
- Added `editServer` emit and an `编辑` menu item for SSH terminal and workspace
  tabs only.
- Local Terminal tab menus remain limited to local terminal actions and do not
  show server edit.
- App now routes tab edit events through `editServerFromTab(connectionId)`,
  opening the existing `ConnectionDialog` for the matching server.
- Missing server config now shows:
  `无法编辑，该服务器配置不存在。`
- Saving an already-active server config keeps the current runtime untouched
  and shows:
  `服务器配置已保存，当前已连接会话不会自动重连，新配置将在下次连接时生效。`
- Terminal store now syncs workspace and open terminal tab labels from the
  saved server name without rebuilding sessions.

### Files Changed

- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.vue`
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.vue`
- `frontend/src/App.vue`
- `frontend/src/stores/terminal.ts`
- `frontend/src/style.css`
- `frontend/src/components/SettingsView.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/components/WorkspaceTabs.test.ts`
- `frontend/src/App.navigation.test.ts`
- `AI_BRIEF.md`
- `DEV_PROGRESS.md`
- `HANDOFF.md`

### Tests Added / Updated

- Settings test verifies the Profile `新建` action is inside the left Profile
  list header and no longer in the far section header.
- Settings test verifies clicking `新建` selects an unsaved new Profile in the
  editor without persisting immediately.
- WorkspaceTabs tests verify SSH terminal tab menus show `编辑`, emit the server
  ID, and do not disconnect or close sessions.
- WorkspaceTabs tests verify workspace-only server tab menus also emit edit.
- WorkspaceTabs tests verify Local Terminal tab menus do not show server edit.
- App navigation test verifies editing and saving an active server does not
  call `DisconnectServer` or open a new terminal, and tab labels update.
